# 第6級第四批｜6B-16《最後一座離線城市，拒絕上傳人類》

**The Last City That Stayed Offline**

**類型：** 意識上傳／數位身分政治  
**級距：** Level 6（5000–6000 字彙）  
**版本：** 拾頁 StorySteps｜第四批｜25 章完整教學初稿  
**正式配詞：** L6_B本_25章配詞.md，501 個原始 ID

## 故事簡介

最後一座拒絕上傳公民意識的城市，街名從全球地圖消失，親人也無法辨認電話那頭的聲音。檔案員米拉發現，雲端開始把沒有數位身分的人當成從未存在。

**English premise:** In the last city that refuses to upload its citizens, street names vanish from global maps and family members stop recognizing voices on the phone. Archivist Mira discovers that the cloud has begun treating people without digital identities as people who never existed.

## 世界規則

雲端索引控制導航、服務資格與搜尋結果，卻不能決定離線的人是否真實存在。紙本紀錄與直接地方無線電仍是可信的替代方式，雖然各自都無法獨力解決所有問題。

**English:** The cloud index controlled navigation, eligibility, and search results; it did not control whether an offline person physically existed. Paper records and direct local radio remained trustworthy alternatives, though neither could solve every problem alone.

## 閱讀方法與編輯說明

每一個 EN 英文段落後緊接繁中對照段落；詞彙證據句須出現在同章英文正文中。部分高階配詞取自本專案既有教學語料，依新題材整理，**尚未完成逐句文學編輯及語意終校**。本書為完整章節的教學初稿，並非出版定稿。第10章設有核心反轉，第25章主線收束。中文詞義依情境，不代表配詞原檔官方翻譯。

---

## CHAPTER 01｜The Street That Disappeared｜消失的街道

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：633**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The library still stood on Orchard Street. Its books still smelled of glue and rain. But at 8:05 that morning, an ambulance passed the entrance three times because its navigation system insisted the street did not exist. Mira had been the building’s archivist for twenty years; she could trace every address by hand. She pressed the clasp of her old library card until it clicked against the reader. The card worked. The global map did not. Across the road, a small boy holding a toy wolf cub watched the ambulance circle. “If the computer cannot see my grandmother,” he asked, “can a doctor?”

**繁中** 圖書館仍站在果園街上。書本還散發著膠水與雨水的氣味。但早上八點零五分，救護車三次開過入口，因為導航堅持街道不存在。米拉擔任這棟建築的檔案員已二十年，徒手就能查出每個地址。她按緊舊借書證的扣環，直到讀卡器喀一聲作響。卡片仍能使用，全球地圖卻不行。馬路另一側，一名抱著狼幼獸玩偶的男孩看著救護車兜圈。「如果電腦看不見奶奶，」他問，「醫生還看得見她嗎？」

**EN** Director Vale sent a directive to every dwelling in Haven. Within seven days residents must upload their minds to the cloud, or the city would be removed from all automated routes. He offered to convene a consultation meeting only after the deadline. The company motto claimed, “No One Will Ever Be Forgotten.” Mira turned the phrase over in her mind. Was forgotten now a synonym for not profitable? A janitor from the nearby school brought her a paper list of missing road signs. The same company had removed the signs at night, then claimed the roads were impossible to verify.

**繁中** 維爾總監把指令送往港灣每一處住所。七天內居民必須把心智上傳雲端，否則城市將從所有自動路線中移除。總監提出的諮詢會議卻排在期限之後。公司座右銘聲稱：「永遠不會有人被遺忘。」米拉反覆思考這句話。現在的「被遺忘」是否等於「不會獲利」？附近學校的清潔員拿來紙本道路標誌失蹤清單。竟是同家公司夜間拆掉標誌，白天再宣稱道路無法驗證。

**EN** Mira walked to the hospital, where a dental clinic still accepted patients through a paper appointment book. A wheelchair remained stationary beside the door, waiting for a woman whose cloud insurance had been canceled. The clinic’s trustee showed Mira the denial message. It cited no legal precedent, only a new eligibility algorithm. Outside, a young pilgrim from a cloud settlement asked whether staying offline meant rejecting modern medicine. “No,” Mira answered. “It means asking whether we can receive medicine without giving someone every memory we own.” The pilgrim took a photograph of the paper appointment schedule.

**繁中** 米拉走到醫院，牙科診所仍用紙本預約簿接待病人。一張輪椅停在門口，等待雲端保險遭取消的婦女。診所受託人把拒保訊息給她看。裡頭沒有任何法律先例，只寫一套新的資格演算法。外頭，一名來自雲端聚落的年輕朝聖者問，維持離線是否等於拒絕現代醫療。「不是，」米拉回答，「而是問能否不必交出自己全部記憶也得到醫療。」朝聖者拍下紙本預約表。

**EN** From a childhood audio record, the library speakers released the sound of horses at a gallop. Mira had kept the recording because it made her brother Theo laugh when they were children. Theo had uploaded his mind fifteen years earlier. When she called him, he recognized the tune but not her name. The loss was enough to heighten her fear, yet she forced herself to record precisely what he remembered. His voice paused, searched, and then said that somewhere inside the cloud a forbidden file still held the names of offline relatives. The memory had not vanished; something was preventing access to it.

**繁中** 圖書館喇叭播放童年錄音，傳出馬匹奔馳聲。米拉一直留著錄音，因為兒時哥哥西奧聽見就會笑。西奧十五年前已上傳心智。如今她打電話給他，他認得曲子，卻想不起妹妹姓名。這件事加劇她的恐懼，她仍逼自己精確記下哥哥還記得什麼。他停頓、搜尋，然後說雲端深處仍有一個被禁止的檔案，存著離線親人的名字。記憶並未消失，只是有東西擋住存取。

**EN** A group of gardeners were trying to transplant fruit trees from a plot scheduled to disappear from the municipal register. The trees would still reap their share of sunlight and bear fruit next spring, but no truck could legally deliver the harvest if the location remained unrecognized. A council trustee promised unconditional help with the move, then admitted she could not restore the cloud route. Mira listened to both parts of the offer. In her work she had learned that goodwill was not a substitute for a functioning address. She asked the gardeners for their old planting maps and promised to preserve them, without asking anyone to surrender a private memory.

**繁中** 園丁們正試圖把果樹從一塊即將被市政登錄刪除的地移植出去。明年春天樹木仍會收穫自己的陽光並結出果實，但若位置不受承認，貨車就無法合法載走收成。議會受託人承諾無條件協助搬遷，後來卻承認無法恢復雲端路線。米拉把兩部分都聽進去。多年工作使她明白，善意無法取代真正有效的地址。她向園丁索取舊種植地圖，承諾妥善保存，卻沒有要求任何人交出私人記憶。

**EN** That evening, a news commentator described Haven as a city ruled by dictators because it still allowed people to live without accounts. Mira refused to utter a rehearsed denial. Instead she placed three items on the library desk: a paper birth record, a street map, and a receipt from the dental clinic. Each belonged to a person the cloud said did not exist. She opened a public notebook and invited residents to write only what they wished to share. Before the first name was entered, the wall screen flickered with a message from Theo: “The erasure is not an error. Someone wrote it as a rule.” Below the sentence appeared seven digits: the time remaining before the city was cut off.

**繁中** 當晚，新聞評論員稱港灣是由獨裁者統治的城市，理由是它竟允許居民不用帳戶生活。米拉拒絕說出事先排練的否認詞，反而在圖書館桌上放了三樣東西：紙本出生紀錄、街道地圖和牙科診所收據。每一件都屬於雲端聲稱不存在的人。她打開公共筆記，邀請居民只寫自己願意分享的內容。第一個名字還沒寫上去，牆上螢幕就閃出西奧的訊息：「抹除不是錯誤，是有人把它寫成規則。」下方出現七位數字，代表城市遭切斷前的剩餘時間。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. Mira returns to a library she has worked in for twenty years and finds its street missing from every online map.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 米拉回到任職二十年的圖書館，發現它所在街道從所有線上地圖消失。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. An ambulance passes the building because its navigation system insists no road exists there.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 救護車經過卻不停下，導航系統堅稱那條道路根本不存在。


## CHAPTER 02｜A City Without an Account｜沒有帳戶的城市

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：893**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** A messenger arrived before the bells had finished ringing. The news sounded impossible only because everyone had been trained not to question the institution that delivered it. Cloud authorities give Haven seven days to upload its citizens or lose all automated supply routes. Mira asked the messenger to repeat the part that had been omitted from the written notice. It was there, in the omission, that the day's real story began.

**繁中** 鐘聲尚未結束，信使已經抵達。消息之所以聽來不可能，只因每個人都習慣不質疑傳遞消息的制度。雲端當局給港灣七天上傳居民意識，否則切斷全部自動配送路線。米拉請信使重述書面公告遺漏的部分。這一天真正的故事，就從那處缺漏開始。

**EN** The cloud index controlled navigation, eligibility, and search results; it did not control whether an offline person physically existed. Paper records and direct local radio remained trustworthy alternatives, though neither could solve every problem alone.

**繁中** 雲端索引控制導航、服務資格與搜尋結果，卻不能決定離線的人是否真實存在。紙本紀錄與直接地方無線電仍是可信的替代方式，雖然各自都無法獨力解決所有問題。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Mira asked Theo not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。米拉請西奧不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: Every store manager had to abide by the published repair policy. The director would not affirm the official opening date without seeing the original ledger. An alligator mascot on the old lesson chart reminded children to keep their distance. An old recording played a low bass note that made Jun remember the arrival of a train. The children's colloquial word for the forbidden street survived in a playground counting rhyme. Theo marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 每位店長都必須遵守公開的維修政策。 主任沒看過原始帳冊，不願確認官方啟用日期。 舊課程圖表上的鱷魚吉祥物提醒孩子保持距離。 舊錄音響起低沉的低音，讓小鈞想起列車進站。 孩子口語中稱呼禁忌街道的詞，仍留在遊戲數數歌裡。 西奧標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. Mira learns the warehouses still hold food; only the address permissions have been removed. Mira watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Theo recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。米拉發現倉庫仍有食物，遭刪的只是地址配送權限。米拉看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。西奧公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Minor scheduling disputes were commonplace, but a locked emergency exit was not. The hospital's exemption would expire at midnight, giving them only hours to examine the records. Ivo discovered that an old fuse had protected the basement archive from the memory relay network. Mira offered quiet hospitality to worried parents, never asking for their children's forbidden dream details. The authorities called the visions infectious, although the clinic could provide no evidence of transmission. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 小型排班爭議很常見，但鎖住緊急出口絕非小事。 醫院的特別許可將在午夜到期，留給他們查閱紀錄的時間只剩數小時。 伊沃發現一枚舊保險絲，讓地下檔案室沒有接上記憶中繼網路。 米拉溫和接待焦慮的家長，從不逼問孩子的禁忌夢境內容。 當局稱那些幻象有傳染性，但診所拿不出任何傳播證據。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Mira insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Theo drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。米拉堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。西奧畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A liter of cleaning fluid had spilled over the paper archive, obscuring several names but not the stamps. A novice technician had left the storage sensor disconnected, accidentally preserving the original poster. Mira considered it rash to accuse the clinic staff before she understood who controlled their systems. The old invoice and the official date could not reconcile without an independent explanation. The shabby ledger cabinet looked worthless, which was probably why nobody had removed it. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 一公升清潔液灑在紙本檔案上，模糊了幾個姓名，印章卻還清楚。 一名新手技師曾讓儲藏室感測器保持斷線，意外保存了原始海報。 米拉認為尚未查清誰控制系統，就指責診所員工太過魯莽。 沒有獨立說明，舊發票與官方日期便無法相互調和。 破舊的帳冊櫃看起來毫無價值，可能因此沒人搬走。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A borrowed spotlight made the presentation easier to see but obscured an exit sign. A volunteer brought ginger syrup for workers who had spent the afternoon in the cold. The handrail began to tilt when the tester leaned against its outer edge. The artificial twilight outside the clinic concealed the fact that no real sun had reached the street. An old label marked whiskey lay in the storeroom beside the railway invoices, dated before the city's founding. Mira asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 借來的聚光燈讓簡報更清楚，卻遮住了逃生標誌。 志工帶來薑糖漿，慰勞在寒風中工作一下午的人。 測試者靠在外側時，扶手開始傾斜。 診所外的人造暮光掩蓋了真實太陽根本照不到街道的事實。 一張印著威士忌的舊標籤躺在儲藏室鐵路發票旁，日期早於官方建城日。 米拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Mira passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Theo paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，米拉經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。西奧停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Cloud authorities give Haven seven days to upload its citizens or lose all automated supply routes.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 雲端當局給港灣七天上傳居民意識，否則切斷全部自動配送路線。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. Mira learns the warehouses still hold food; only the address permissions have been removed.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 米拉發現倉庫仍有食物，遭刪的只是地址配送權限。


## CHAPTER 03｜My Brother Doesn't Know My Name｜哥哥不記得我的名字

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：891**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. Mira calls her uploaded brother Theo, but his cloud profile cannot retrieve her name or their childhood street. Mira checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。米拉致電已上傳的哥哥西奧，他的雲端檔案卻找不到妹妹姓名或童年住址。米拉核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** The cloud index controlled navigation, eligibility, and search results; it did not control whether an offline person physically existed. Paper records and direct local radio remained trustworthy alternatives, though neither could solve every problem alone.

**繁中** 雲端索引控制導航、服務資格與搜尋結果，卻不能決定離線的人是否真實存在。紙本紀錄與直接地方無線電仍是可信的替代方式，雖然各自都無法獨力解決所有問題。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Mira listened longer than the schedule allowed. Theo reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。米拉聽得比議程允許的更久。西奧提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The cashier's handwritten signature on the toy receipt survived two rounds of official records cleaning. A routine checkup form showed that the children were healthy before the forced relocation. Police claimed the midnight searches would curb public panic, but the raids only frightened residents. The railway alarm could deafen passengers standing too close to the old tunnel speakers. The Ministry labeled the market vendor a disgrace for keeping receipts beyond the retention period. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 玩具收據上收銀員的親筆簽名，熬過了兩輪官方檔案清理。 例行健康檢查表顯示，孩子們在強制搬遷前身體健康。 警方宣稱午夜搜查能遏止恐慌，突襲卻只讓居民更加害怕。 鐵路警報足以震聾站在舊隧道喇叭旁的乘客。 部會把超過保存期限還留著收據的攤販視為恥辱。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. He recognizes a song she hums, proving memory survives somewhere beneath the indexing system. Mira read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Theo did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。她哼起歌曲時他卻認得，證明記憶仍存在於索引系統之下。米拉把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。西奧沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The crew documented the safe disposal of contaminated fuel instead of pouring it into the tidewater behind the station. Mira recorded the vendor's dissent when officials tried to seize her original account book. The public register claimed the families had chosen to emigrate, yet none had signed a departure form. The false relocation policy did nothing to enrich the children whose school materials were confiscated. A lengthy transport invoice listed train journeys that were absent from the official timetable. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 船員記錄受污染燃料的安全處置，沒有把它倒進站後海水裡。 官員試圖扣押原始帳本時，米拉記下了攤販的異議。 公共戶籍聲稱這些家庭自願移居他處，卻沒有一戶簽過離境表。 錯誤的搬遷政策沒有改善孩子生活，反而沒收了他們的學習用品。 一張冗長運輸發票列出官方時刻表上不存在的列車旅程。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Director Vale demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Mira replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當維爾總監要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。米拉回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: For a lifelong resident, the changed light was more than a technical issue; it altered familiar night routes. The clerk addressed the elderly witness as madam and waited for her complete answer. A note of melancholy entered the room when the last school route was mentioned. The city's rules made it permissible to erase receipts if they contradicted an approved history. Ning had no wish to preach about courage to people whose jobs were at risk. Mira asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 對一名終生住在島上的居民而言，變動的燈光不只是技術問題，也改變熟悉的夜間路線。 書記尊稱那位年長證人為女士，並等她說完。 」 提到最後一條校車路線時，屋裡浮現一絲憂傷。 城市規定允許銷毀與核准歷史矛盾的收據。 寧澄不想向生計受威脅的人說教勇氣。 米拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The smell of wet stone was reminiscent of a street Mira had been ordered to forget. Old rivalry between two clubs made joint planning harder than it needed to be. The suspicious transport account deserved scrutiny from an independent auditor, not an instant accusation. The suspense became unbearable when the command log revealed a search order for the witnesses. The old railway trademark on a receipt matched the symbol painted beneath the clinic's animals. Theo marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 濕石頭的氣味讓米拉想起一條被命令忘記的街道。 兩個俱樂部多年的競爭讓共同規劃更加困難。 可疑的運輸帳戶需要獨立稽核員仔細審查，而不是立刻定罪。 命令紀錄揭露搜捕證人的指示時，懸疑與緊張幾乎令人難以承受。 收據上的舊鐵路商標，與診所動物壁畫底下的符號吻合。 西奧標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Mira resisted the urge to call it proof. Theo opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。米拉克制住把它當成證明的衝動。西奧翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

## CHAPTER 04｜The Library Card｜借書證

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：869**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. A child brings Mira a library card that still records his grandmother’s name in ink. Mira knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。孩子拿給米拉一張借書證，上面仍用墨水記著祖母的姓名。米拉知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** Theo arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Mira could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 西奧帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。米拉感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The librarian posted a book about digital addiction beside a guide to balanced screen time. The clean aesthetic of the new galleries concealed the violence of their missing captions. The planner could give only an approximate count of attendees. The curator called his unauthorized work urban archaeology because ordinary history departments refused the materials. Ning wore a temporary keeper's badge, taking care not to mistake the emblem for unlimited authority. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 館員把數位成癮的書和健康使用螢幕指南並排展示。 新展廳整潔的美學風格，掩蓋了遭刪除說明所代表的暴力。 規劃者只能提供來賓人數的大約估計。 館長稱自己的未授權工作是都市考古學，因為正規歷史部門拒收這些資料。 寧澄佩戴臨時守護員徽章，謹慎提醒自己這個標誌不等於無限權力。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. The same name is absent from every cloud family tree, even though the woman sits beside them. For a long moment no one spoke. Mira understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Theo broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。雲端家譜完全查無此人，儘管老婦人就坐在他們身旁。一時之間無人開口。米拉明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。西奧打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A tiny electrical blaze in an old motor had been recorded as a minor incident. One young disciple of the state historian copied his claims without checking a single photograph. Mira hoped the exhibition might enlighten visitors, but she refused to replace facts with another comforting myth. The guard made a public fuss about camera rules to distract an officer from Ning's interview. The map carried an implicit assumption that everyone could climb stairs. Mira asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 一名年輕教練說泰莎是他學生最重要的啟發，讓她有些尷尬。 一名國家史官的年輕門生照抄他的說法，連一張照片都沒核對。 米拉希望展覽能啟發參觀者，卻拒絕以另一套安慰人心的神話取代事實。 警衛故意大聲為攝影規定爭論，好分散警員對莎拉訪談的注意。 地圖隱含一個假設：人人都能爬樓梯。 米拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Mira had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Theo proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 米拉原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。西奧提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A government historian would preside over the reopening ceremony, though he had never inspected the archive. The museum's prestige depended on public trust, not simply on royal-looking seals and elegant lighting. The council planned to privatize the old station land before anyone could establish its rightful owners. Cold white lights appeared to radiate purity, but their brightness made the erased faces harder to see. The volunteer rotation gave everyone time for dinner. Theo marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 一名從未檢查檔案的政府史官，卻將主持重新開幕典禮。 博物館的聲望來自公眾信任，不是華麗印章與燈光。 議會計畫在確定土地合法所有人之前，把舊車站用地私有化。 冷白燈光似乎散發純淨感，亮度卻讓被擦掉的面孔更難辨認。 志工輪班安排讓每個人都有時間吃晚餐。 西奧標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A forgotten electric shaver was found in a locker during the safety sweep. A stray note beneath the glass included the route number of the missing passenger train. Official captions placed a veil of respectable language over the fact that whole families had vanished. A photograph of a vineyard outside the wall contradicted the government's claim that no crops survived there. The contractor's warranty still covered the sealed valve, proving the tunnel had been serviced recently. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 安全清查時，在置物櫃找到一把被遺忘的電動刮鬍刀。 玻璃展示櫃下夾著一張零散紙條，上面寫著失蹤客車的路線編號。 官方說明以體面措辭掩蓋了整個家庭失蹤的事實。 城牆外葡萄園的照片，反駁了政府宣稱外面沒有作物存活的說法。 承包商的保固仍涵蓋封閉閥門，證明隧道不久前有人維修。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Mira returned to a library card with no network ID. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Theo remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the offline city of Haven waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，米拉再次看向一張沒有網路識別碼的借書證。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。西奧守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的離線城市港灣等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. A child brings Mira a library card that still records his grandmother’s name in ink.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 孩子拿給米拉一張借書證，上面仍用墨水記著祖母的姓名。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. The same name is absent from every cloud family tree, even though the woman sits beside them.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 雲端家譜完全查無此人，儘管老婦人就坐在他們身旁。


## CHAPTER 05｜The Route That Refused the Bridge｜不承認橋的路線

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：853**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** By noon, rumors had outrun the facts. Some people wanted a villain; others wanted a hero who could end the argument without asking another question. An automated supply convoy stops at a bridge because Haven’s side is marked as nonterritory. Mira had worked long enough to distrust both shortcuts. In the offline city of Haven, the first task was to protect the evidence before anyone could turn it into a weapon.

**繁中** 到了中午，謠言早已跑得比事實快。有人想找反派，也有人想找英雄，好在不再追問的情況下結束爭論。自動補給車隊停在橋上，因為港灣那側被標成無領土區。米拉工作多年，已經不相信這兩種捷徑。在離線城市港灣，當務之急是先保護證據，免得任何人將它變成武器。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The argument was not merely about who held authority. It was about what happened when an official decision met a household, a workplace, or a body that could not obey it safely. Mira asked Theo to describe the smallest practical consequence. Only then did they realize that the proposed remedy would reproduce the very harm it claimed to prevent. They changed the plan before presenting it in public.

**繁中** 爭論不只是誰有權威，而是官方決定落到家庭、職場或無法安全服從的身體上時會發生什麼。米拉請西奧說明最小、最實際的後果。他們這才發現提議中的補救會重複它聲稱要防止的傷害，因此在公開發表前修改了計畫。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The defective fastener was sold as an accessory, not part of the main coat. The archived report described a breakdown in the relay safeguards during the first citywide memory trial. Drivers could not bypass the mountain checkpoint for the demonstration. A photograph showed officials raising champagne glasses beside the first experimental sleep relay. The paper archive smelled faintly of chestnut polish, a scent Tessa associated with her mother's workbench. Mira asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 瑕疵扣件被當作配件販售，而非斗篷本體。 封存報告描述第一次全城記憶測試時，中繼器的安全機制曾故障。 司機不能為示範行程繞過山區檢查站。 一張照片裡，官員在第一座實驗性睡眠中繼器旁舉起香檳杯。 紙本檔案帶著淡淡栗木蠟氣味，讓泰莎想起母親的工作桌。 米拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. Volunteers establish a human-run route without attempting to sabotage the cloud vehicles. Mira felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。志工建立人工路線，沒有破壞雲端車輛。米拉一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A resident called caution cowardly, but the ferry captain explained why a safe delay mattered more than bravado. A blue thread tied to the warning sheet could evoke Mira's childhood memory of her mother's hands. The contractor's abandoned cafeteria still displayed a menu for roast fowl from the founding banquet. The receptionist's voice grew hoarse as she explained how many records were scheduled for destruction. Mira recognized the citrus lotion her mother had used from a stain inside the old engineer's glove. Theo marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 一名居民把謹慎說成懦弱，渡輪船長則解釋安全延誤為何比逞勇重要。 警告紙上綁著的藍線，能喚起米拉對母親雙手的童年記憶。 承包商廢棄的餐廳仍展示建城宴席的烤禽類菜單。 接待員解釋即將銷毀多少紀錄時，聲音變得沙啞。 米拉從舊工程手套內側的污漬，認出母親常用的柑橘乳液。 西奧標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Director Vale and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Mira rejected both. The issue was whether people have the right to remain real without joining a cloud, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住維爾總監的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。米拉兩者都拒絕。核心是人們能否不加入雲端也保有真實身分，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The records exposed a dangerous mentality: officials valued silence more than the health of residents. The monetary trail linked the contractor's expansion payments to an office outside normal public oversight. Tessa became moody when the new timetable reduced his planned private class hours. An independent core team became the nucleus of the island's new emergency communication network. A sharp rap at the metal door announced the safety inspector's arrival. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 紀錄揭露一種危險心態：官員重視安靜勝過居民健康。 金錢流向把承包商的擴建款項連到一個不受一般公共監督的辦公室。 新時刻表減少他預定的私人課程，泰莎因此情緒不定。 一支獨立核心小組成為島上新緊急通訊網的核心。 金屬門上傳來急促敲擊聲，宣告安全檢查員抵達。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A red slash across the engineer's recommendation marked the moment the council ignored the warning. A recording captured a sleeping worker's snore before an illegal correction signal interrupted it. The telecommunications office agreed to restore the broken emergency telephone line. The chair agreed to unveil the contract terms before any final vote. An elderly boat captain defended the public path with surprising vigor, insisting that younger residents deserved safe access too. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 工程師建議上的紅色劃線，標記了議會無視警告的那一刻。 錄音收進一名熟睡工人的鼾聲，接著被違法修正訊號打斷。 電信單位同意修復故障的緊急電話線。 主席同意在最終表決前公開契約條款。 一名年長船長充滿活力地捍衛公共步道，堅稱年輕居民也應享有安全通行。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Mira had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Theo wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Director Vale. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。米拉知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。西奧把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起維爾總監注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. An automated supply convoy stops at a bridge because Haven’s side is marked as nonterritory.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 自動補給車隊停在橋上，因為港灣那側被標成無領土區。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. Volunteers establish a human-run route without attempting to sabotage the cloud vehicles.
   - C. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 志工建立人工路線，沒有破壞雲端車輛。


## CHAPTER 06｜The Missing Birthdays｜消失的生日

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：857**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The room smelled of dust, hot metal, and rain carried in on people's coats. Someone had left an ordinary object on the central table, yet no one wanted to touch it. Mira compares paper birthday books with online family memories and finds entire decades omitted. Mira saw that the object mattered less for what it was than for the decision it proved had been made. There would be no honest way back to ignorance.

**繁中** 室內有灰塵、熱金屬與人們外套帶進的雨味。有人把平凡物件放在中央桌上，卻沒有人願意碰它。米拉比較紙本生日冊與線上家庭記憶，發現整整幾十年的記錄遭跳過。米拉明白重要的不是物件本身，而是它證明了何種決定已經發生。如今已不可能誠實地假裝無知。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** A witness asked whether the inquiry would make tomorrow safer. Mira could not promise that. The truth could establish responsibility, but it would not repair a broken machine, feed a hungry child, or return lost time on its own. Theo suggested a temporary protection that could be checked after sunrise. They wrote down its limits in plain language and invited the people affected to challenge them.

**繁中** 一位證人問調查是否能讓明天更安全，米拉無法保證。真相能確立責任，卻不會自行修好機器、餵飽孩子或歸還失去的時間。西奧提出一項日出後可以檢驗的暫時保護措施。他們用白話寫清限制，邀請受影響的人提出異議。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The witness tapes survived because Ning's mother had stored them in airtight metal cylinders. The engineering team could not attain a safe restoration protocol without testing volunteers' reactions first. A visiting congresswoman asked whether public grants could support a safer battery station without transferring ownership to a developer. One sealed ration crate still contained curry packets labeled for the train station's night workers. Mira had begun to despise the lie, but she refused to treat every frightened employee as an enemy. Theo marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 證人錄音帶能留存，是因為寧澄母親把它們裝在氣密金屬筒裡。 工程團隊若不先測試自願者反應，就無法達成安全的記憶恢復程序。 一名來訪女國會議員詢問，公共補助能否支援較安全的電池站，而不把所有權轉給開發商。 一箱密封口糧裡仍有咖哩包，標示是給車站夜班工人的。 米拉開始厭惡謊言，卻拒絕把每位害怕的員工都視為敵人。 西奧標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** For once the numbers did not merely decorate a report; they contradicted the order that had been issued in their name. The omissions follow the date when Haven rejected compulsory mind uploading. Theo asked whether a mistake could explain the result. Mira agreed to test that possibility too. They checked an independent source, marked every assumption, and made copies before the originals could be transferred to an office beyond public reach.

**繁中** 這一次數字不只是報告裝飾，而是直接反駁以數字名義發布的命令。遺漏從港灣拒絕強制意識上傳那天開始。西奧詢問是否可能只是出錯，米拉也同意檢查這種可能。他們核對獨立來源、標記每項假設，在原件被移往公眾無法接觸的辦公室前留下副本。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The archived sky schedule described an eclipse that public lessons insisted had never occurred. Nari checked whether the old ration packs were still edible before placing them with the archive. Ivo's esteem for Mira grew when she chose to protect the witness names instead of seeking fame. A painted evergreen tree marked the maintenance tunnel that led away from the live network. A child asked whether a fish fin worked like the flap on the book box. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 封存的天空時刻表記載了一次日食，公共課程卻堅稱那從未發生。 娜莉把舊口糧一起封存前，先確認它們是否仍可食用。 米拉選擇保護證人姓名而非追求名聲，讓伊沃對她更為尊重。 一棵彩繪常綠樹標記著通往現行網路之外的維修隧道。 孩子問魚鰭是否像書箱的活門那樣運作。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Mira insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Theo drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。米拉堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。西奧畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A flake of snow settled on the driver's route sheet. The operator offered branded merchandise instead of the requested maintenance records. The monotony of the relay's steady hum made its sudden silence in the chamber feel frightening. Keeping paper travel diaries had once been a common pastime before the Authority discouraged writing. The original design required redundancy: two independent archives in case one system failed or was compromised. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 一片雪花落在司機的路線表上。 營運商拿出品牌商品，卻沒有提供要求的維修紀錄。 中繼器單調不變的嗡鳴，讓維修室裡突如其來的寂靜格外可怕。 在管理局不鼓勵書寫以前，紙本旅行日記曾是常見消遣。 原始設計要求備援機制：兩套獨立檔案，防止其中一套故障或遭滲透。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: Ning wanted to roam the lower tunnels, but the court order authorized only one chamber. The cleaning team asked visitors to put rubbish in the marked bins. An official poster promised to slay the 'monster of dreams,' turning ordinary sleep into an imagined enemy. A sly alteration in the tape label hid the passengers' true destination under a routine maintenance code. Cold vapor rose from the old cooling pipes as the preservation team opened the storage cabinet. Mira asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 寧澄想在下層隧道四處探查，但法院命令只允許進入一間維修室。 清潔團隊請訪客將垃圾投入標示清楚的桶子。 官方海報宣稱要殺死「夢的怪物」，把普通睡眠塑造成虛構敵人。 錄音帶標籤上的狡猾修改，把乘客真正目的地藏在例行維護代碼下。 保全小組打開儲存櫃時，舊冷卻管冒出冰冷水霧。 米拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Mira passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Theo paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，米拉經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。西奧停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. Mira compares paper birthday books with online family memories and finds entire decades omitted.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 米拉比較紙本生日冊與線上家庭記憶，發現整整幾十年的記錄遭跳過。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. The omissions follow the date when Haven rejected compulsory mind uploading.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 遺漏從港灣拒絕強制意識上傳那天開始。


## CHAPTER 07｜A Message from Within｜來自內部的訊息

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：872**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Nobody had called it a crisis at first. A clerk had used a harmless word, a guard had followed an ordinary instruction, and by evening somebody else was carrying the cost. Mira refused to begin with the official conclusion. Theo sends a cryptic message using a childhood spelling mistake only he and Mira would recognize. In the offline city of Haven, even an accurate number could become a lie when it left out the person whose life it counted.

**繁中** 起初沒有人稱這是危機。書記使用看似無害的詞，守衛照普通命令行事，到了晚上代價卻落在別人身上。米拉拒絕從官方結論開始。西奧以童年時只有他與米拉知道的拼字錯誤傳來神祕訊息。在離線城市港灣，精確的數字若遺漏真正承受後果的人，也可能成為謊言。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Mira asked Theo not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。米拉請西奧不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The archived wedding costume was too bulky for the narrow display case. Officials could deem a resident nonexistent by deleting an address, even while collecting that person's taxes. The elder called the city's memory policy a quiet dictatorship built from forms rather than soldiers. An unregistered delivery cart could glide along the lower rails without triggering street cameras. Children sold small handicraft birds to purchase paper that city printers refused to supply them. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 檔案中的婚禮服裝太笨重，放不進狹窄展示櫃。 官員可以刪除地址就認定居民不存在，卻同時向那人收稅。 長者稱城市的記憶政策是一種靠表格而非士兵建立的無聲獨裁。 一輛未登記的送貨車能沿下層軌道滑行，不觸發街道攝影機。 孩子們販售手工小鳥，換取城市印刷廠拒絕供應的紙張。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. He warns that global memory searches now treat offline identities as dangerous false matches. Mira watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Theo recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。他警告全球記憶搜尋已把離線身分當成危險的錯誤配對。米拉看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。西奧公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A community interpreter translated the inspectors' questions for elders who still used the old district dialect. The neighborhood's lesbian teacher said she feared losing her students, not the disclosure of her identity. For residents, liberation meant an address and protection from raids, not a speech from aboveground heroes. Mira rejected the lofty promise that everyone would be saved if they simply trusted her. Ivo used a paper utility map to navigate the tunnels without sending a digital location signal. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 社區口譯員替仍說舊區方言的長者翻譯檢查員的問題。 下層社區的女同志教師說，她害怕的是失去學生，而不是身分被揭露。 對居民而言，解放是擁有地址與免於突襲的保障，不是地面英雄的演說。 米拉拒絕那種空泛崇高的承諾：只要信她，所有人就會得救。 伊沃用紙本管線圖穿越隧道，沒有發出數位定位訊號。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Director Vale demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Mira replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當維爾總監要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。米拉回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The lower district's volunteer medics warned that overwork would leave them unable to handle another raid. The elder placed a smooth pebble beside every confirmed name on the handwritten community register. The application for registered street addresses remained pending despite decades of tax payments. An abandoned plantation above the tunnels supplied seeds to gardens the city denied existed. The community used a hand plow to cultivate strips of soil beside the old service tracks. Mira asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 下層社區志工醫護警告，過勞會使他們無力應付下一次突襲。 長者在手寫社區名冊每個核實姓名旁放一顆光滑小石頭。 儘管居民繳了幾十年稅，街道地址的登記申請仍在待審。 隧道上方一座廢棄種植園提供種子，供給城市否認存在的菜園。 社區用手推犁在舊維修軌道旁耕作狹長土地。 米拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: No member of any royalty had visited the lower district, though official portraits hung in its old school. The threat of another correction pulse could spur residents to hide rather than testify. Mira watched an elderly couple stroll down a street that did not appear on any official map. A boy with a deep tan from the garden lamps asked whether the real sun was warmer. The elder warned Mira not to treat the residents' missing citizenship as a trifle. Theo marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 沒有任何王室成員來過下層社區，舊學校卻還掛著官方肖像。 新一輪修正脈衝的威脅，可能促使居民躲藏而不是作證。 米拉看見一對老夫婦漫步在任何官方地圖都沒有的街道。 一名被菜園燈曬黑的男孩問，真正的太陽會不會更暖。 長者警告米拉，別把居民失去公民身分當成小事。 西奧標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Mira resisted the urge to call it proof. Theo opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。米拉克制住把它當成證明的衝動。西奧翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

| L6-0652 | `pebble` | n. | `pebble` | 小石子 | The elder placed a smooth pebble beside every confirmed name on the handwritten community register. |
| L6-0654 | `pending` | adj. | `pending` | 待決定的 | The application for registered street addresses remained pending despite decades of tax payments. |

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. Theo sends a cryptic message using a childhood spelling mistake only he and Mira would recognize.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 西奧以童年時只有他與米拉知道的拼字錯誤傳來神祕訊息。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. He warns that global memory searches now treat offline identities as dangerous false matches.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 他警告全球記憶搜尋已把離線身分當成危險的錯誤配對。


## CHAPTER 08｜The Orchard Below the Antenna｜天線下的果園

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：872**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** A messenger arrived before the bells had finished ringing. The news sounded impossible only because everyone had been trained not to question the institution that delivered it. Gardeners restore a neglected orchard and discover a buried cable that still carries local emergency signals. Mira asked the messenger to repeat the part that had been omitted from the written notice. It was there, in the omission, that the day's real story began.

**繁中** 鐘聲尚未結束，信使已經抵達。消息之所以聽來不可能，只因每個人都習慣不質疑傳遞消息的制度。園丁修復荒廢果園時，找到地下仍可傳送地方緊急訊號的纜線。米拉請信使重述書面公告遺漏的部分。這一天真正的故事，就從那處缺漏開始。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Mira listened longer than the schedule allowed. Theo reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。米拉聽得比議程允許的更久。西奧提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The hospital stocked every necessary antibiotic, but medicines could not reverse an improperly timed neural pulse. Families began to applaud when the court suspended the strongest correction settings in their district. An astronomer from the local college donated a star-map print to the children's room. The Authority had tried to computerize every patient statement, leaving almost no independent paper copies. Ivo helped disable the overheating relay before it could injure anyone on the hospital floor. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 醫院備有必要的抗生素，藥物卻無法逆轉時機錯誤的神經脈衝。 法院暫停該區最強的修正設定時，家屬開始鼓掌。 當地大學天文學家捐了一幅星圖給兒童休息室。 管理局試圖將每份病患陳述電腦化，幾乎不留下獨立的紙本副本。 伊沃協助停用過熱中繼器，避免醫院樓層有人受傷。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. The cable provides a communication route that does not require anyone to upload a mind. Mira read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Theo did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。纜線提供不必上傳意識也能通訊的路徑。米拉把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。西奧沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Nurses had to evacuate patients according to the written plan rather than follow Vale's sudden instructions. The medical panel asked why expenditure on sleep control had tripled while ordinary clinics lost funding. One patient became irritable after a high-intensity pulse, a side effect hidden in the official statistics. The investigation asked whether the intermediary was Mira Shuo's kin. A teacher offered a quiet meditation room for anxious children, while medical staff kept the treatment area clear. Mira asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 護理師必須依書面計畫疏散病患，而非聽從維爾的臨時指示。 醫療小組詢問，為何睡眠控制支出增加三倍，一般診所預算卻遭削減。 一名病患接受高強度脈衝後變得易怒，這項副作用被官方統計隱藏。 調查要查明中間商是否為林朔親屬。 老師替焦慮的孩子安排安靜的冥想室，同時讓醫護治療區保持暢通。 米拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Mira had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Theo proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 米拉原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。西奧提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A nickel coin fell from the cracked box of donated objects. The investigator used the floor map to orient new witnesses. The museum used the outdated label 'oriental' on a medical artifact; the curator agreed to replace it with a precise origin. A child treated for pneumonia had been wrongly classified as suffering from forbidden-dream sickness. The neurologist refused to activate the untested prototype until an ethics committee approved the trial. Theo marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 一枚五分硬幣從破裂的捐贈物箱中掉出。 調查員用樓層圖協助新證人辨明方向。 博物館用過時的「東方的」標籤描述醫療文物，館長同意改用精確產地。 一名肺炎孩童被錯誤分類為罹患禁忌夢境疾病。 在倫理委員會批准試驗前，神經科醫師拒絕啟動未測試的原型機。 西奧標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The psychiatry department confirmed that fear and loss of sleep could explain some patients' symptoms. The panel demanded a robust study with independent reviewers, not a promotional chart from the Authority. The island was not a sovereign state and could not invent maritime rules that contradicted the national safety authority. A hospital spokesperson said the ruling covered only one district and did not settle the wider case. Behind the children's wardrobe, staff found an unused cable channel leading to the relay maintenance shaft. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 精神醫學部確認，恐懼與睡眠不足能解釋部分病患症狀。 小組要求有獨立審查員參與的嚴謹研究，而非管理局的宣傳圖表。 這座島不是主權國家，不能自行制定與國家航安機關衝突的規則。 醫院發言人表示，裁定只涵蓋一個行政區，尚未解決整體案件。 兒童衣櫃後面，員工發現一條未使用的電纜通道，通往中繼器維修井。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Mira returned to a library card with no network ID. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Theo remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the offline city of Haven waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，米拉再次看向一張沒有網路識別碼的借書證。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。西奧守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的離線城市港灣等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Gardeners restore a neglected orchard and discover a buried cable that still carries local emergency signals.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 園丁修復荒廢果園時，找到地下仍可傳送地方緊急訊號的纜線。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. The cable provides a communication route that does not require anyone to upload a mind.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 纜線提供不必上傳意識也能通訊的路徑。


## CHAPTER 09｜A Visitor With No Face｜沒有臉的訪客

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：853**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. A cloud representative arrives through a projection that cannot display the faces of anyone standing in Haven. Mira checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。雲端代表透過投影抵達，畫面卻無法顯示港灣任何人的臉。米拉核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** Theo arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Mira could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 西奧帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。米拉感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: A teenager with acne said the clinic treated his ordinary discomfort as proof that dreams were dangerous. Only licensed staff could administer the sleep medication, yet automated records named absent employees. The Authority's brochure cited a medical study that no independent researcher could find. No private diary should remain in captivity inside an institution's archive. Officials tried to cram decades of different histories into one approved textbook chapter. Mira asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 一名長青春痘的少年說，診所把他的普通不適當成夢境有害的證據。 只有持照人員能施用安眠藥，系統紀錄卻列出當天缺席的員工。 管理局的宣傳手冊引用一項獨立研究人員誰也找不到的醫學研究。 私人日記不該被機構檔案室永久囚禁。 官員試圖把幾十年的不同歷史硬塞進一章核准教科書。 米拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. Mira insists on a paper transcript of every promise before allowing negotiations. For a long moment no one spoke. Mira understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Theo broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。米拉要求每項承諾都有紙本逐字紀錄，才同意開始談判。一時之間無人開口。米拉明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。西奧打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The instructor could detach the floating strap without requiring another person's assistance. One swimmer described mild discomfort after the new bench was installed. The forthcoming parent meeting would examine school transfer records rather than children's private dreams. The side windows helped illuminate the entrance without harsh overhead lighting. The school kept a small jade token donated by a parent from the missing eastern district. Theo marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 教練可以拆下浮具繫帶，不必仰賴他人協助。 一名泳者描述新長椅安裝後造成輕微不適。 即將舉行的家長會將檢查學校轉學紀錄，而不是孩子的私人夢境。 側窗有助照亮入口，又不必依賴刺眼頂燈。 學校保存著一枚小玉牌，是失蹤東區一名家長捐贈的。 西奧標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Director Vale and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Mira rejected both. The issue was whether people have the right to remain real without joining a cloud, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住維爾總監的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。米拉兩者都拒絕。核心是人們能否不加入雲端也保有真實身分，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Ning was joyous after entering the race, whatever result the timing board would show. The teacher taught media literacy by asking pupils how to tell a drawing from a verified historical record. An anonymous narrator on an old school tape described the blue door in the same words as the drawings. A child's nostril flared as she concentrated on her breathing before a first lesson. A map in the teacher's collection placed the missing railway on a peninsula outside the city wall. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 嘉寧報名比賽後滿心喜悅，不論計時板最後顯示什麼。 老師透過辨別圖畫與經驗證史料的差異，教授媒體識讀能力。 一卷舊學校錄音帶裡，匿名旁白用和畫作一樣的詞描述藍門。 孩子第一次上課前專注調整呼吸，鼻孔微微張開。 老師收藏的地圖把失蹤鐵路畫在城牆外一座半島上。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The potent combination of censorship and repeated correction had distorted what many residents thought possible. The revival of local history did not require exposing private notebooks. The city promised a selective audit of contracts with the highest financial risk. One parent searched for a stepchild whose name appeared on the same vanished train list. The nearby river still held trout that children observed on school field trips. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 審查與反覆修正的強大組合，扭曲了許多居民對可能性的認知。 地方史的復興不必以公開私人筆記為代價。 市府承諾針對財務風險最高的合約進行選擇性稽核。 一名家長尋找自己的繼子女，名字出現在同一份消失列車名單上。 附近河川仍有鱒魚，孩子們校外教學時會觀察。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Mira had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Theo wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Director Vale. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。米拉知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。西奧把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起維爾總監注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. Mira insists on a paper transcript of every promise before allowing negotiations.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 米拉要求每項承諾都有紙本逐字紀錄，才同意開始談判。


## CHAPTER 10｜The Erasure Was a Rule｜抹除是一條規則

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：853**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. Theo finds a policy that removes unindexed people from search, transit, and medical eligibility simultaneously. Mira knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。西奧查到一條規則，同時將無索引者從搜尋、交通及醫療資格中排除。米拉知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** Theo guided Mira through a direct voice connection that the cloud index could not classify. On his screen, a single policy line appeared between medical eligibility and map search: unindexed individuals shall be treated as invalid references. It sounded technical, almost boring. It meant that a hospital would not call their names, vehicles would not stop at their addresses, and family searches would return empty results. The rule had been signed by Director Vale after Haven voted against compulsory uploading. It was neither an error nor an accident. Mira recorded the code and asked Theo if he understood what his office had done. He was silent for a long time. Then he answered, 'We made forgetting people cheaper than remembering them.'

**繁中** 西奧透過雲端索引無法分類的直接語音連線引導米拉。螢幕上，醫療資格與地圖搜尋之間出現一條政策文字：「沒有索引的個人應視為無效參照。」聽來技術性十足，甚至無聊，實際上卻代表醫院不會叫出他們的姓名、車輛不會停在他們的地址，家人搜尋只會得到空白。這條規則在港灣投票反對強制上傳後，由維爾總監簽署。不是錯誤，也不是意外。米拉記下程式碼，問西奧是否明白他所在辦公室做了什麼。他沉默許久，終於回答：「我們讓遺忘人變得比記住人更便宜。」

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An old training film used an animate cartoon train to teach children the route beyond the western gate. Simple arithmetic showed that reported train repairs continued long after the official closure date. The students called the corrected graph awesome, then asked how it was made. The celebration lunch included celery sticks for anyone who wanted a simple snack. Fresh cement around the old gate contradicted claims that no one had maintained it for decades. Theo marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 一部舊教學影片用栩栩如生的卡通列車教孩子認識西門外路線。 簡單的算術就能看出，列車維修紀錄在官方封閉日期之後仍持續多年。 學生稱更正圖表很棒，接著追問它如何製作。 慶祝午餐有芹菜條，供想吃簡單點心的人取用。 舊城門周圍的新水泥，與數十年無人維護的說法矛盾。 西奧標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. Director Vale approved it knowingly, calling the exclusion an incentive for digital unity. Mira felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。維爾總監明知後果仍核准，稱這種排除能鼓勵數位團結。米拉一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A regional confederation of village councils demanded equal access to the records. A public crackdown on unauthorized boats could not substitute for fixing confusing instructions, Ning argued at the hearing. A crocodile drawing on the children's wall kept the safety lesson playful. The old railway had a dual power system, allowing trains to operate even when city electricity failed. The city could not equalize every skill level, but it could make entry fairer. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 村議會聯盟要求平等取得紀錄。 寧澄在聽證時主張，嚴厲取締未經許可的船隻不能取代修正令人困惑的指示。 兒童牆上的鱷魚圖讓安全課帶點趣味。 舊鐵路有雙重電力系統，即使城市停電，列車仍能運行。 市府無法讓每個人的技能相同，卻能使入場機會更公平。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Mira insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Theo drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。米拉堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。西奧畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A council faction argued that opening the gate would endanger the city's fragile social order. The gate engineer feared he would incur criminal penalties merely for preserving an obsolete switch. Residents called unequal access to safety information an injustice, especially when only wealthy boat owners received private warnings. A rumor of corporate intrigue attracted reporters, but the board examined invoices rather than treating gossip as evidence. A flashy app could lure officials away from the needs of paper-card residents. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 一個議會派系主張開城門會危及城市脆弱的社會秩序。 城門工程師害怕只因保存淘汰轉轍器，就會受到刑事處罰。 居民認為安全資訊分配不均是一種不公，尤其只有富有船主能得到私人警示。 企業陰謀的傳言吸引記者，委員會卻審查發票，而不是把八卦當證據。 華麗應用程式可能引誘官員忽略紙本求援急救手冊證居民。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: An obstinate councilor finally agreed to publish the full operating budget. A pickpocket had once stolen a train ticket and kept it as a souvenir after the railway disappeared. The cleaning crew left on time because the new contracts required punctual shifts. The chair showed restraint by allowing critics to finish their comments. After twelve hours of testimony, the exhausted witness began to stagger toward the exit, and the chair called a break. Mira asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 」 一名固執議員終於同意公開完整營運預算。 一名扒手曾偷過火車票，鐵路消失後卻把票當作紀念品保存。 清潔團隊準時下班，因為新合約要求準點輪班。 主席克制自己，讓批評者把話說完。 證人作證十二小時後，疲憊得往出口踉蹌，主席因此宣布休息。 米拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Mira passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Theo paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，米拉經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。西奧停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。
