## CHAPTER 11｜The Paper Census｜紙本人口普查

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：839**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** By noon, rumors had outrun the facts. Some people wanted a villain; others wanted a hero who could end the argument without asking another question. Mira organizes a door-to-door census recording who lives in Haven without requiring any network identifier. Mira had worked long enough to distrust both shortcuts. In the offline city of Haven, the first task was to protect the evidence before anyone could turn it into a weapon.

**繁中** 到了中午，謠言早已跑得比事實快。有人想找反派，也有人想找英雄，好在不再追問的情況下結束爭論。米拉組織挨家挨戶的人口普查，不要求任何網路識別碼也能記錄居民。米拉工作多年，已經不相信這兩種捷徑。在離線城市港灣，當務之急是先保護證據，免得任何人將它變成武器。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** A witness asked whether the inquiry would make tomorrow safer. Mira could not promise that. The truth could establish responsibility, but it would not repair a broken machine, feed a hungry child, or return lost time on its own. Theo suggested a temporary protection that could be checked after sunrise. They wrote down its limits in plain language and invited the people affected to challenge them.

**繁中** 一位證人問調查是否能讓明天更安全，米拉無法保證。真相能確立責任，卻不會自行修好機器、餵飽孩子或歸還失去的時間。西奧提出一項日出後可以檢驗的暫時保護措施。他們用白話寫清限制，邀請受影響的人提出異議。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The family archive had won international acclaim before the payment questions emerged. Mira acknowledged the ambiguity of dream imagery instead of pretending that every vision was accurate. The report ignored bodily limits that had been stated in the original medical note. A cold draught crossed the hearing room when someone opened the ventilation hatch. The archived machine log measured heat in Fahrenheit, unlike the city's present metric settings. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 付款疑雲浮現前，家族檔案曾享有國際讚譽。 米拉承認夢境影像的歧義，不假裝每個幻象都準確。 報告忽略原始醫療紀錄中已明列的身體限制。 有人打開通風口時，一股冷風穿過聽證室。 封存機械紀錄用華氏溫度量測熱能，與現行公制設定不同。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** For once the numbers did not merely decorate a report; they contradicted the order that had been issued in their name. A young man refuses to share his full history; his right to privacy is respected. Theo asked whether a mistake could explain the result. Mira agreed to test that possibility too. They checked an independent source, marked every assumption, and made copies before the originals could be transferred to an office beyond public reach.

**繁中** 這一次數字不只是報告裝飾，而是直接反駁以數字名義發布的命令。一名年輕人拒絕提供全部經歷，普查員尊重他的隱私。西奧詢問是否可能只是出錯，米拉也同意檢查這種可能。他們核對獨立來源、標記每項假設，在原件被移往公眾無法接觸的辦公室前留下副本。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The archive's fireproof cabinet held a copy of the disputed training record. A geographical discrepancy placed the old station west of a wall the official map said had always existed. The committee found no evidence that a hacker had changed the archived document. Even a layman could see that the contract granted indefinite data rights. A court could liberate the records from secrecy, but it could not decide what every resident must remember. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 檔案室防火櫃保存著爭議訓練紀錄的複本。 地理上的矛盾，把舊車站定位在官方宣稱一直存在的城牆西側。 委員會沒有發現駭客修改封存文件的證據。 即使外行人也看得出契約授予無限期資料使用權。 法院可以讓紀錄擺脫保密，卻不能決定每位居民必須記得什麼。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Director Vale demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Mira replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當維爾總監要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。米拉回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A mermaid mural from the lost railway appeared on two photographs taken decades apart. The seized archive included miscellaneous receipts that proved surprisingly important to the timeline. Each occurrence of an unusual login had to be recorded independently. A family outing ticket listed a train route that the government had later erased from transit maps. Mira chose to persevere through testimony rather than make an unsupported accusation for dramatic effect. Mira asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 失蹤鐵路上的美人魚壁畫，出現在相隔幾十年拍攝的兩張照片裡。 查扣檔案裡有各種雜項收據，對重建時間線竟出乎意料重要。 每次異常登入事件都必須獨立記錄。 一張家庭出遊車票印有後來遭政府從運輸圖抹除的列車路線。 米拉選擇持續提出證詞，而非為了戲劇效果做出無根據的指控。 米拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The judge asked the court to ponder how a government could collect taxes from an officially nonexistent district. The hearing began to revolve around verifiable records instead of arguments about whose dreams felt convincing. Tessa put a clean utensil beside the tea, keeping food away from documents. Ning warned that labeling Ning a villain before the inquiry ended would not replace evidence. A market yogurt container carried a printed delivery address from the supposedly destroyed station district. Theo marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 法官請法庭思考：政府怎能向官方不存在的行政區收稅？ 聽證逐漸圍繞可查的紀錄，而不是爭論誰的夢感覺更真實。 宋穎把乾淨餐具放在茶旁，讓食物遠離文件。 寧澄提醒，調查尚未結束前先把寧澄叫做惡人，不能取代證據。 一個市場優格容器印著據稱已毀車站區的送貨地址。 西奧標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Mira resisted the urge to call it proof. Theo opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。米拉克制住把它當成證明的衝動。西奧翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Mira organizes a door-to-door census recording who lives in Haven without requiring any network identifier.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 米拉組織挨家挨戶的人口普查，不要求任何網路識別碼也能記錄居民。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. A young man refuses to share his full history; his right to privacy is respected.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 一名年輕人拒絕提供全部經歷，普查員尊重他的隱私。


## CHAPTER 12｜The Last Analog Hospital｜最後的類比醫院

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：859**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The room smelled of dust, hot metal, and rain carried in on people's coats. Someone had left an ordinary object on the central table, yet no one wanted to touch it. The local hospital treats cloud and offline patients alike, using paper labels and spoken identification. Mira saw that the object mattered less for what it was than for the decision it proved had been made. There would be no honest way back to ignorance.

**繁中** 室內有灰塵、熱金屬與人們外套帶進的雨味。有人把平凡物件放在中央桌上，卻沒有人願意碰它。地方醫院用紙本標籤和口頭確認，同等照護雲端與離線病人。米拉明白重要的不是物件本身，而是它證明了何種決定已經發生。如今已不可能誠實地假裝無知。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Mira asked Theo not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。米拉請西奧不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: A laboratory attendant identified the storage shelves where the children's original treatment registers had been kept. The cook stirred pancake batter while two engineers argued about clocks at the kitchen table beside her. The most recent island census helped officials estimate how many households needed printed emergency notices. A retired chemist explained how old ink could be dated without damaging the children's records. A small bird began to chirp outside the laboratory window, briefly interrupting the engineers' debate over satellite time. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 一名實驗室服務員指出，兒童原始治療名冊存放在哪些架上。 廚師攪拌鬆餅麵糊時，兩名工程師在旁邊餐桌爭論時鐘。 最新島上人口普查協助官員估算有多少家庭需要紙本緊急通知。 退休化學家解釋如何不損傷兒童紀錄，就能測定舊墨水年代。 小鳥在實驗室窗外啁啾，短暫打斷工程師對衛星時間的辯論。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. A cloud insurer denies reimbursement, prompting a separate access complaint. Mira watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Theo recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。雲端保險公司拒絕理賠，因而引發獨立的就醫權申訴。米拉看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。西奧公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The volunteer desk could dispense blankets only after the inventory was recorded. The technician let out a groan when he discovered that the latest digital copy lacked entire pages. The missing laboratory files were only the visible tip of an iceberg of altered civic records. The undamaged consent forms proved invaluable for distinguishing voluntary treatment from compulsory correction. Ning had funded a lavish celebration of safe sleep while cutting staff assigned to monitor side effects. Mira asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 駕駛疲憊的呻吟讓泰莎要求休息。 技師發現最新數位副本缺了整頁時，發出一聲呻吟。 失蹤的實驗室檔案，只是整批遭修改市政紀錄露出的冰山一角。 完好的同意書對區分自願治療與強制修正極其珍貴。 寧澄砸錢舉辦奢華安眠慶典，卻削減追蹤副作用的人力。 米拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Mira had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Theo proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 米拉原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。西奧提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The city's new logo had to appear on the public inspection notices. A loudspeaker ordered visitors to leave, but the judge's preservation warrant kept the examination team inside. New martial security regulations threatened to transfer the medical archive to an armed enforcement office. No confirmed outbreak preceded the children's transfers, despite what the founding textbooks claimed. A preserved oyster shell in Mira's childhood box suggested a visit to the coast before the city sealed its gates. Theo marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 公開檢查公告必須使用市府新標誌。 擴音器命令訪客離開，但法官的保全令使檢查小組得以留下。 新的軍事化安全規章威脅要把醫療檔案移交武裝執法辦公室。 儘管建城教科書如此宣稱，兒童轉移之前沒有任何確診疫情爆發。 梅童年盒子裡保存的牡蠣殼，顯示城門封鎖前她可能去過海岸。 西奧標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The early engineers never intended the technology to populate every home with compulsory monitors. The fallen tree was a setback, not a reason to abandon the service. Some residents remained skeptical of the proposed licensed detour. Visitors could subscribe to updates without buying the collection. The charity promised not to withhold apprentice wages during an audit. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 早期工程師從未打算讓強制監測環遍布每戶家庭。 倒木是挫折，不是放棄服務的理由。 部分居民仍懷疑提議中的合格繞道路線。 來賓不必購買系列，也能訂閱更新資訊。 慈善機構承諾稽核期間不扣留學徒薪水。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Mira returned to a library card with no network ID. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Theo remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the offline city of Haven waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，米拉再次看向一張沒有網路識別碼的借書證。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。西奧守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的離線城市港灣等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The local hospital treats cloud and offline patients alike, using paper labels and spoken identification.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 地方醫院用紙本標籤和口頭確認，同等照護雲端與離線病人。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. A cloud insurer denies reimbursement, prompting a separate access complaint.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 雲端保險公司拒絕理賠，因而引發獨立的就醫權申訴。


## CHAPTER 13｜The Cost of a Forgotten Town｜被遺忘城鎮的代價

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Nobody had called it a crisis at first. A clerk had used a harmless word, a guard had followed an ordinary instruction, and by evening somebody else was carrying the cost. Mira refused to begin with the official conclusion. Mira documents businesses that lost trade after automated maps stopped showing Haven. In the offline city of Haven, even an accurate number could become a lie when it left out the person whose life it counted.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。米拉聽得比議程允許的更久。西奧提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

A licensed electrician showed the council how local relays could be isolated without cutting hospital power. A tear clung to the mother's eyelash as she described forgetting her son's laughter. Mira asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. The evidence establishes that digital erasure can cause physical harm without a single weapon. **EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Director Vale and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Mira rejected both. The issue was whether people have the right to remain real without joining a cloud, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住維爾總監的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。米拉兩者都拒絕。核心是人們能否不加入雲端也保有真實身分，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

The proposed safeguard was not reliant on a single network administrator or political leader. The sanitation team checked shower areas between the morning and afternoon sessions. The council advertised scenic views from the dome while hiding the living neighborhoods below it. The emergency plan required engineers to open each relay cabinet's mechanical shutter before isolation. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 舊螢幕可放大成績，讓後排坐著的人看清楚。 提議的防護措施不依賴單一網路管理員或政治領袖。 衛生團隊在早、午場之間檢查淋浴區。 議會宣傳穹頂的美麗風景，卻藏起下方仍有人居住的社區。 緊急計畫要求工程師在隔離前打開每個中繼櫃的機械百葉門。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The guild needed to stabilize its records before restarting production. Vale's stylish presentation could not answer the question of whether citizens had consented. The coalition offered to supplement its medical petition with authenticated records from affected families. The coalition requested suspension of the strongest pulses, not compulsory erasure of existing memories. The speaker slowed her tempo when she explained that recovering history must respect individual choice. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 恢復生產前，公會得先穩定紀錄系統。 維爾時髦精美的簡報，仍答不出市民是否同意的問題。 聯盟願意以受影響家庭的認證紀錄補充醫療請願。 聯盟要求暫停最強脈衝，而不是強制抹除既有記憶。 發言者解釋恢復歷史必須尊重個人選擇時，放慢了語速。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Mira had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. ### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. Mira documents businesses that lost trade after automated maps stopped showing Haven.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 米拉記錄自動地圖停止顯示港灣後，企業失去交易的損失。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. The evidence establishes that digital erasure can cause physical harm without a single weapon.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 證據證明數位抹除即使沒有武器，也能造成實體傷害。


## CHAPTER 14｜A Voice That Knows Me｜認得我的聲音

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：848**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** A messenger arrived before the bells had finished ringing. The news sounded impossible only because everyone had been trained not to question the institution that delivered it. Theo recognizes Mira during a direct audio connection that bypasses the cloud’s personal index. Mira asked the messenger to repeat the part that had been omitted from the written notice. It was there, in the omission, that the day's real story began.

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

Nari opposed a charitable publicity event that offered food only to residents willing to endorse Vale. The family had treated undocumented design purchases as customary practice. The museum's modest endowment was enough for document storage, not for rebuilding the entire viewing platform. Theo marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 由於各社區中繼硬體不同，停機程序需要因地調整。 受託人討論長期修復方案時，伊拉先替樓梯扶手加裝鋼支架。 娜莉反對一場以支持維爾換取食物的慈善宣傳活動。 家族一直把未記錄的設計採購當成慣例。 博物館有限的捐贈基金足夠保存文件，卻無法重建整座觀景平台。 西奧標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. He confesses that he once helped design the rule that now suppresses her identity. For a long moment no one spoke. Theo broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A reflective vest made the crossing monitor visible at dusk. **繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 反光背心讓渡口監看員在黃昏時清楚可見。 護理師向等待夜間醫療觀察的居民提供茶點。 淘汰中繼面板上有一個爬蟲形標記，與孩子們的鱷魚海報吻合。 面對自己工程成果的審查前，米拉需要一小時獨處。 維爾安排一連串忠誠副手，準備在議會停職他時繼續捍衛網路。 米拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Mira passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Theo paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，米拉經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。西奧停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

## CHAPTER 15｜The City Votes to Stay｜城市投票留下

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：850**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. Haven residents vote to remain offline while agreeing to share only the minimum data needed for emergency access. Mira checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。港灣居民投票維持離線，同意只分享緊急通行所需的最低限度資料。米拉核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The argument was not merely about who held authority. It was about what happened when an official decision met a household, a workplace, or a body that could not obey it safely. Mira asked Theo to describe the smallest practical consequence. Only then did they realize that the proposed remedy would reproduce the very harm it claimed to prevent. They changed the plan before presenting it in public.

**繁中** 爭論不只是誰有權威，而是官方決定落到家庭、職場或無法安全服從的身體上時會發生什麼。米拉請西奧說明最小、最實際的後果。他們這才發現提議中的補救會重複它聲稱要防止的傷害，因此在公開發表前修改了計畫。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: A senior official smoked a cigar while claiming the archived safety warnings had never been serious. The temporary closure remained in force for the damaged pump corridor. The mapped descent to the hidden archive began beneath the lower district's abandoned pumping station. The Authority had tried to dispose of obsolete consoles before investigators could copy their physical logs. The town hoped to diversify its economy beyond summer tourism, including maritime education and safer ferry services. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 一名高階官員抽著雪茄，聲稱封存的安全警告從未值得重視。 受損幫浦走廊的臨時關閉令仍然有效。 地圖標示通往隱藏檔案的下行通道，起點在下層社區廢棄抽水站下方。 管理局曾企圖丟棄淘汰控制台，搶在調查員複製實體紀錄以前。 小鎮希望使經濟多元化，不只依賴夏季觀光，也發展海事教育與更安全的渡輪服務。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. The result rejects both isolation and compulsory uploading. Mira felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。結果同時拒絕徹底隔絕與強制上傳。米拉一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Ning had learned to excel at software optimization yet failed to question the institutions deploying her work. Her fascination with engineering had once kept her from noticing whose interests the system served. Vale became a political foe, but Mira refused to make hatred of him the foundation of reform. The canal's artificial stars continued to glitter even as citizens questioned the story of the dome. The city's official astronomy model depicted only one hemisphere, concealing observations from beyond the wall. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 寧澄擅長軟體最佳化，卻沒質疑使用她作品的機構。 她對工程的著迷，曾使她忽略系統究竟服務誰的利益。 維爾成為政治對手，米拉卻拒絕以仇恨他作為改革基礎。 市民開始質疑穹頂故事時，運河上的人造星光仍閃閃發亮。 城市官方天文模型只呈現一個半球，隱藏了城牆外的觀測。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Director Vale demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Mira replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當維爾總監要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。米拉回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Innumerable small edits had accumulated across the population, making restoration a medical as well as legal challenge. Mastery of the software did not make the operator an expert on village roads. The notorious correction program had been presented as a harmless treatment for disordered sleep. The panel rejected outright the claim that Ning's negligence made all other documents false. The state postponed the premiere of its promotional film until after the independent hearing. Mira asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 無數細微修改在全體居民身上累積，使恢復同時成為醫療與法律難題。 精通軟體不代表操作員懂得村莊道路。 惡名昭彰的修正計畫，起初被包裝成治療睡眠混亂的無害方法。 小組斷然駁回「寧澄疏失使其他文件全屬虛假」的說法。 國家把宣傳影片首映延到獨立聽證之後。 米拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: Ning tried to seduce skeptical officials with promises of stability and renewed funding. A sealed report mentioned a slaughter during the old evacuation, but investigators needed corroboration before assigning responsibility. The missing district's ration records suggested deliberate shortages, though they did not yet prove starvation. One retired statesman agreed to authenticate the old council signatures without claiming to remember every meeting. The tedious comparison of approval logs revealed exactly when Ning's patch had been expanded without consent. Theo marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 寧澄試圖以穩定與新預算承諾誘惑持懷疑態度的官員。 一份密封報告提到舊撤離時的屠殺，但調查員仍需交叉證據才能判定責任。 失蹤行政區的配給紀錄顯示可能有刻意短缺，卻尚不能證實有人餓死。 一名退休政治家同意鑑定舊議會簽名，卻不宣稱記得每場會議。 繁瑣比對核准紀錄，揭露寧澄的修補程式何時在未經同意下被擴張。 西奧標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Mira resisted the urge to call it proof. Theo opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。米拉克制住把它當成證明的衝動。西奧翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

## CHAPTER 16｜A Trial of Two Memories｜兩種記憶的審判

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：872**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. Independent experts compare Theo’s cloud memory with paper records and physical witnesses. Mira knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。獨立專家比較西奧的雲端記憶、紙本紀錄與實體證人。米拉知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** A witness asked whether the inquiry would make tomorrow safer. Mira could not promise that. The truth could establish responsibility, but it would not repair a broken machine, feed a hungry child, or return lost time on its own. Theo suggested a temporary protection that could be checked after sunrise. They wrote down its limits in plain language and invited the people affected to challenge them.

**繁中** 一位證人問調查是否能讓明天更安全，米拉無法保證。真相能確立責任，卻不會自行修好機器、餵飽孩子或歸還失去的時間。西奧提出一項日出後可以檢驗的暫時保護措施。他們用白話寫清限制，邀請受影響的人提出異議。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: Dust had begun to accumulate around the empty cabinet where a heavy archive box had recently stood. Beginners needed time to accustom themselves to the temperature and sound of the room. Ning recognized an affectionate note from her mother beside the original school roster. The old astronomy passage survived because it had been omitted from the modern relay network. Ira limited caffeine during the long watch and scheduled breaks, knowing alert workers also needed sleep. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 一只大檔案箱不久前還放著的空櫃周圍，灰塵開始堆積。 初學者需要時間習慣館內溫度與聲響。 寧澄在原始學校名冊旁認出母親寫的一句充滿愛意的話。 舊天文通道得以保存，因為它未接入現代中繼網路。 伊拉在漫長值班時控制咖啡因攝取並安排休息，知道警覺的工人也需要睡眠。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** For once the numbers did not merely decorate a report; they contradicted the order that had been issued in their name. They show that a cloud index is useful but cannot be the sole judge of whether a person exists. Theo asked whether a mistake could explain the result. Mira agreed to test that possibility too. They checked an independent source, marked every assumption, and made copies before the originals could be transferred to an office beyond public reach.

**繁中** 這一次數字不只是報告裝飾，而是直接反駁以數字名義發布的命令。他們證明雲端索引有用，卻不能成為判斷人是否存在的唯一依據。西奧詢問是否可能只是出錯，米拉也同意檢查這種可能。他們核對獨立來源、標記每項假設，在原件被移往公眾無法接觸的辦公室前留下副本。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Investigators found a clone of the official map in which deleted towns were visible under older names. A former national contestant joined the new class as an ordinary learner. The map was not definitive proof of deportation, but it gave investigators verifiable locations to inspect. Fresh detergent stains on the empty cabinet suggested that someone had cleaned it only days earlier. The archive volunteers needed endurance, not dramatic courage, to compare thousands of handwritten entries. Mira asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 調查員找到一份官方地圖複製版，被刪城鎮在上面仍以舊名出現。 一名曾代表國家參賽的選手以普通學員身分加入新班。 地圖不能成為強制遷移的最終證明，卻提供了可實地查驗的地點。 空櫃上的新清潔劑污漬，顯示有人幾天前才擦洗過。 檔案志工比對數千筆手寫紀錄，靠的是耐力而非戲劇化勇氣。 米拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Mira had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Theo proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 米拉原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。西奧提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The structural engineer used a mechanical gauge to test pressure before opening the old hatch. An old photograph of a school goalkeeper helped date a class roster from the missing district. Council members wanted to modernize the archive, but residents insisted that paper originals remain protected. A child's lunch note mentioning pickled radish matched a recipe preserved by the lower district elder. The realization that both adults had applied for distant work led to an unexpectedly cheerful discussion of separate plans. Theo marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 結構工程師在打開舊艙門前，用機械儀表測試壓力。 一張學校守門員的舊照片，協助判定失蹤行政區班級名冊的年代。 議員希望讓檔案現代化，居民卻堅持保護紙本正本。 一張提到醃蘿蔔的兒童午餐字條，與下層社區長者保存的食譜吻合。 兩人都申請外地工作這個領悟，反而讓他們愉快地討論各自計畫。 西奧標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: An old textbook blamed socialism for vanished towns, though its political claims lacked supporting documents. Personal recognition was subjective, so the surveyor checked every landmark against independent measurements. Ning could sympathize with workers facing layoffs while still requiring the operator to disclose the failed sensor. The old school medical ward appeared on the map despite being absent from the current city registry. Ning felt weary after the meeting and chose to rest rather than drive. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 一本舊課本把城鎮消失歸咎於社會主義，政治說法卻缺乏文件支持。 個人認得的感覺很主觀，因此測量員用獨立數據核對每處地標。 寧澄能同情面臨裁員的工人，仍要求營運商揭露感測器失效。 舊學校醫療病房出現在地圖上，現行城市戶籍卻沒有它。 會後寧澄感到疲倦，選擇休息而非開車。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Mira returned to a library card with no network ID. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Theo remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the offline city of Haven waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，米拉再次看向一張沒有網路識別碼的借書證。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。西奧守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的離線城市港灣等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. Independent experts compare Theo’s cloud memory with paper records and physical witnesses.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 獨立專家比較西奧的雲端記憶、紙本紀錄與實體證人。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. They show that a cloud index is useful but cannot be the sole judge of whether a person exists.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 他們證明雲端索引有用，卻不能成為判斷人是否存在的唯一依據。


## CHAPTER 17｜The Consent Switch｜同意開關

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：833**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** By noon, rumors had outrun the facts. Some people wanted a villain; others wanted a hero who could end the argument without asking another question. Engineers test an opt-in identity bridge that lets residents access services without uploading consciousness. Mira had worked long enough to distrust both shortcuts. In the offline city of Haven, the first task was to protect the evidence before anyone could turn it into a weapon.

**繁中** 到了中午，謠言早已跑得比事實快。有人想找反派，也有人想找英雄，好在不再追問的情況下結束爭論。工程師測試自願加入的身分橋接，讓居民不必上傳意識也能使用服務。米拉工作多年，已經不相信這兩種捷徑。在離線城市港灣，當務之急是先保護證據，免得任何人將它變成武器。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Mira asked Theo not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。米拉請西奧不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: Mira translated the network's abstraction of memory patterns into practical risks that judges could understand. Medical teams checked each patient's airway before attributing any symptoms to the correction pulse. The restored archive would amplify voices previously excluded from its history. A sealed data capsule held the unauthorized test pulse log for independent examination. The curfew barred ordinary travel after midnight but did not cancel court-authorized safety inspections. Mira asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 米拉把網路對記憶模式的抽象描述，轉成法官能理解的實際風險。 醫療團隊先檢查每名病患的呼吸道，再判斷症狀是否與修正脈衝有關。 修復後的檔案將放大過去被排除的聲音。 一個密封資料膠囊保存未授權測試脈衝紀錄，以供獨立查核。 宵禁禁止一般居民午夜後外出，卻沒有取消法院授權的安全檢查。 米拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. A resident revokes permission and the bridge obeys without erasing her medical history. Mira watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Theo recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。居民撤銷授權後，橋接系統遵守要求，也沒有抹除其醫療紀錄。米拉看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。西奧公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The residents' peaceful defiance consisted of keeping written records despite the revision minister's warnings. A contractor received a dividend while the city reduced funding for the clinics harmed by its network. The driver used geometry to compare turning angles beside the shelter. The volunteers shared grapefruit at the break, keeping food outside the records room. Officials hoped repeated curfews would harden citizens' fear into habitual obedience. Theo marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 居民的和平反抗，是即使歷史修正部長警告，仍繼續保存書面紀錄。 一家承包商領了股利，城市卻削減受到網路傷害的診所預算。 司機用幾何計算比較閱覽棚旁的轉彎角度。 志工休息時分享葡萄柚，將食物留在檔案室外。 官員希望反覆宵禁能讓市民恐懼固化成習慣性服從。 西奧標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Director Vale and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Mira rejected both. The issue was whether people have the right to remain real without joining a cloud, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住維爾總監的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。米拉兩者都拒絕。核心是人們能否不加入雲端也保有真實身分，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The library offered an honorary title, but the donor wanted voting control. One historical costume depicted a heroine trying to imprison a corrupt ruler. One notable detail in the relay log was the account number used for the unauthorized test. A local playwright offered to document the cloth mages' history with consent. A quiet procession of neighbors accompanied the affected families to the public counter to submit their objections. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 圖書館提出榮譽頭銜，捐助者卻想取得投票控制權。 一件歷史戲服描繪女英雄逃離監禁。 中繼紀錄有一項值得注意的細節：未授權測試使用的帳戶號碼。 本地劇作家提議在取得同意後記錄布術師歷史。 鄰居們組成安靜的隊伍，陪同受影響家庭到公共櫃台提交異議。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The serial number on the opened relay cabinet matched the contractor's maintenance form. A sociology lecturer testified that enforced forgetting had damaged trust between generations. A quiet song seemed to soothe readers anxious about the weather. Reporters began to swarm the courthouse once the unauthorized test became public. The control building's false dawn cast an upward beam across the closed windows. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 被打開的中繼櫃序號與承包商維修表吻合。 社會學講師作證，強制遺忘已破壞世代間的信任。 柔和歌聲似乎安撫了為天氣焦慮的讀者。 未授權測試曝光後，記者開始湧向法院。 控制大樓的人造黎明向上投射一道光，掠過緊閉窗戶。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Mira had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Theo wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Director Vale. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。米拉知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。西奧把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起維爾總監注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

## CHAPTER 18｜The Director's Offer｜總監的提議

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：850**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The room smelled of dust, hot metal, and rain carried in on people's coats. Someone had left an ordinary object on the central table, yet no one wanted to touch it. Vale offers to restore Haven instantly if the city agrees to upload every newborn by default. Mira saw that the object mattered less for what it was than for the decision it proved had been made. There would be no honest way back to ignorance.

**繁中** 室內有灰塵、熱金屬與人們外套帶進的雨味。有人把平凡物件放在中央桌上，卻沒有人願意碰它。維爾提出只要港灣同意新生兒預設上傳，就立刻恢復城市服務。米拉明白重要的不是物件本身，而是它證明了何種決定已經發生。如今已不可能誠實地假裝無知。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Mira listened longer than the schedule allowed. Theo reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。米拉聽得比議程允許的更久。西奧提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An old astronaut training file listed external settlements, contradicting the claim that all humanity lived in Everday. The political breakup of the first Memory Commission left records scattered across several departments. A senior bureaucrat had signed deportation orders while describing them as routine public-health transfers. The auditor used a mechanical calculator to verify the relocation payments independently of digital records. The children in an old photograph looked carefree before officials moved their families without explanation. Theo marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 一份舊太空人訓練檔案列出外部聚落，與全人類只住在三地球走廊的說法矛盾。 首屆記憶委員會的政治解體，使紀錄散落各部門。 一名高階官僚簽署強制遷移命令，卻把它說成例行公共衛生轉移。 稽核員用機械計算機核對遷移款項，不依賴數位紀錄。 舊照片中的孩子看起來無憂無慮，後來官員卻毫無解釋地遷走家庭。 西奧標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. Mira refuses to bargain away the rights of children who cannot consent. Mira read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Theo did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。米拉拒絕拿無法同意的孩子權利交換。米拉把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。西奧沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A possible catastrophe was reason to improve the plan, not create panic. Nari issued a concise public summary that distinguished confirmed facts from unresolved deaths. A courtyard in the lower district held a memorial with names missing from every official register. The ministry hoped a spectacular anniversary ceremony would dazzle citizens into overlooking the archive findings. The rain made the steep incline behind the tower slippery, prompting volunteers to close the footpath temporarily. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 可能發生的災難是改善計畫的理由，不是製造恐慌。 娜莉發表簡明公告，區分已確認事實與尚未釐清的死亡案例。 下層社區一座院子裡有紀念碑，刻著所有官方名冊都沒有的姓名。 部會希望華麗的週年慶能使市民目眩，忽略檔案發現。 雨水使塔後陡坡濕滑，志工因此暫時封閉步道。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Mira insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Theo drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。米拉堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。西奧畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Investigators found a transit locker containing duplicate tickets and letters from evacuees. An official film portrayed the outside settlements as medieval ruins, though their radio equipment was modern. The testimony described how forced relocation had separated families and reshaped experiences of motherhood. A pharmacy record from Harbor Nine matched medicines listed on an Everday evacuation train. Investigators agreed to publicize verified totals without exposing private medical histories. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 調查員找到一個運輸置物櫃，裡面有車票副本及撤離者書信。 官方影片把外部聚落拍成中世紀廢墟，但其無線電設備很現代。 證詞描述強制搬遷如何拆散家庭，改變許多人的母職經驗。 九號港的一份藥局紀錄，與永晝撤離列車名單上的藥物吻合。 調查員同意公布核實總數，不揭露私人病史。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The radiant artificial skyline could no longer conceal the living towns beyond the dome. The old administration's reliance on compulsory correction had concealed accountability for decades. Officials had labeled the lower district a slum to justify neglect, though families maintained schools and clinics there. The anniversary spectacle became meaningless once original relocation orders appeared in public. An old guard watched a child's dog wag its tail beside the sealed station and looked away in shame. Mira asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 明亮的人造天際線再也掩蓋不了穹頂外仍有人居住的城鎮。 舊政府依賴強制修正，讓責任追究被掩蓋數十年。 官員把下層社區稱作貧民窟，以合理化忽視，居民卻自己維持學校和診所。 原始搬遷命令公開後，週年慶的壯觀表演變得毫無意義。 一名老警衛看著孩子的狗在封閉車站旁搖尾巴，羞愧地轉過頭。 米拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Mira passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Theo paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，米拉經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。西奧停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. Vale offers to restore Haven instantly if the city agrees to upload every newborn by default.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 維爾提出只要港灣同意新生兒預設上傳，就立刻恢復城市服務。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. Mira refuses to bargain away the rights of children who cannot consent.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 米拉拒絕拿無法同意的孩子權利交換。


## CHAPTER 19｜The Library Goes Dark｜圖書館停電

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：867**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Nobody had called it a crisis at first. A clerk had used a harmless word, a guard had followed an ordinary instruction, and by evening somebody else was carrying the cost. Mira refused to begin with the official conclusion. A supply denial causes the library’s emergency generator to fail during a winter storm. In the offline city of Haven, even an accurate number could become a lie when it left out the person whose life it counted.

**繁中** 起初沒有人稱這是危機。書記使用看似無害的詞，守衛照普通命令行事，到了晚上代價卻落在別人身上。米拉拒絕從官方結論開始。冬季暴風中，供應遭拒使圖書館緊急發電機故障。在離線城市港灣，精確的數字若遺漏真正承受後果的人，也可能成為謊言。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** Theo arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Mira could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 西奧帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。米拉感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The memorial boulevard displayed the names of survivors who had chosen public recognition. A shopkeeper served hot vegetable broth to crews working through the morning to clear storm debris from the harbor. The tired captain's pale complexion worried the nurse, who asked him to rest before returning to his vessel. A cosmetic repair to the painted railing could wait; structural cracks in the pier could not. A crater outside the old industrial plant appeared on aerial photographs omitted from founding lessons. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 紀念大道展示自願公開姓名的倖存者名單。 店主為整個上午清除港口風暴殘骸的工作隊送上熱蔬菜高湯。 疲憊船長蒼白的臉色讓護理師擔心，請他在回船前先休息。 欄杆油漆的表面修補可以等，碼頭的結構裂縫卻不能等。 舊工業廠外的一個坑洞出現在建城課程省略的空拍照片裡。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. Volunteers bring lanterns and carry books to shelters, keeping the archive accessible. For a long moment no one spoke. Mira understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Theo broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。志工帶來提燈，把書搬往避難所，確保檔案持續可用。一時之間無人開口。米拉明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。西奧打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The captain used voice dictation to record a route change while keeping both hands available for steering. The old disease story had served as a diversion from the industrial land transfer. Mira could choose whether to enroll in the new family-contact program after reviewing its privacy rules. The evidence against the original council was formidable, but investigators still separated each person's actions. A gleam of clean water showed through the newly opened shallow pool window. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 船長使用語音聽寫記錄航線改變，讓雙手仍能操作船舵。 舊疾病故事曾用來轉移人們對工業土地轉讓的注意。 梅可以在閱讀隱私規範後，自行決定要不要加入新的家屬聯絡計畫。 針對舊議會的證據相當有力，調查員仍逐一區分個人行為。 新開的淺水池窗外透出乾淨水面的微光。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Director Vale demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Mira replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當維爾總監要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。米拉回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The new travel policy would remove a bureaucratic handicap that prevented unregistered residents from buying tickets. The independent guardian could intervene if a family contact request ignored a survivor's wishes. A lunar motif appeared in the institute's design brief. The children ate oatmeal before their morning reading session. From the outset, the reunion process required consent from both adults rather than a state order. Mira asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 新交通政策將移除阻止無戶籍居民購票的行政障礙。 若家屬聯絡請求無視倖存者意願，獨立監護人可以介入。 學院設計簡報中出現月亮主題圖案。 孩子晨間閱讀前吃了燕麥粥。 家人重逢程序從一開始就要求兩名成年人同意，而非服從國家命令。 米拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The exhausted diver was warned not to overdo the final inspection merely to meet an optimistic reopening schedule. The maintenance worker paused to sharpen a dull marking pencil before drawing the repair line. The usually sociable shopkeeper grew quiet while counting deliveries that could not reach families during the closure. Mira rejected the idea that engineers possessed moral superiority over the people whose lives they changed. No single victor could claim to have restored the city; thousands of people had preserved its evidence. Theo marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 團隊提醒疲憊潛水員不要為了樂觀的重開日程就過度工作。 維修工人先削尖鈍掉的標記鉛筆，再畫修繕線。 平時健談的店主在計算封航期間無法送到家庭的物資時安靜下來。 米拉拒絕工程師在道德上優於其技術所影響之人的想法。 沒有單一勝利者可以自稱拯救城市；數千人共同保存了證據。 西奧標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Mira resisted the urge to call it proof. Theo opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。米拉克制住把它當成證明的衝動。西奧翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. A supply denial causes the library’s emergency generator to fail during a winter storm.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 冬季暴風中，供應遭拒使圖書館緊急發電機故障。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. Volunteers bring lanterns and carry books to shelters, keeping the archive accessible.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 志工帶來提燈，把書搬往避難所，確保檔案持續可用。


## CHAPTER 20｜The Name in the Source Code｜原始碼裡的名字

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：858**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** A messenger arrived before the bells had finished ringing. The news sounded impossible only because everyone had been trained not to question the institution that delivered it. Theo identifies the original clause allowing people to stay unindexed without losing civil services. Mira asked the messenger to repeat the part that had been omitted from the written notice. It was there, in the omission, that the day's real story began.

**繁中** 鐘聲尚未結束，信使已經抵達。消息之所以聽來不可能，只因每個人都習慣不質疑傳遞消息的制度。西奧在原始碼找到允許人民不建立索引仍保有公共服務的原始條款。米拉請信使重述書面公告遺漏的部分。這一天真正的故事，就從那處缺漏開始。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The argument was not merely about who held authority. It was about what happened when an official decision met a household, a workplace, or a body that could not obey it safely. Mira asked Theo to describe the smallest practical consequence. Only then did they realize that the proposed remedy would reproduce the very harm it claimed to prevent. They changed the plan before presenting it in public.

**繁中** 爭論不只是誰有權威，而是官方決定落到家庭、職場或無法安全服從的身體上時會發生什麼。米拉請西奧說明最小、最實際的後果。他們這才發現提議中的補救會重複它聲稱要防止的傷害，因此在公開發表前修改了計畫。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The electricians installed an aluminum protective shield around an exposed backup cable before isolating a relay. The breadth of the new safety review included maintenance records, human training, public access, and ecological disturbance. A coastal commonwealth offered neutral observers to verify that the city had stopped compulsory corrections. Residents of a crowded dormitory requested a supervised relay shutdown after reviewing their medical options. A newspaper editorial praised the passing trial while reminding readers that ownership questions remained unresolved. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 電工隔離中繼器前，在裸露備援電纜周圍裝了鋁製防護罩。 新安全審查的範圍涵蓋維護紀錄、人員訓練、公共通行與生態干擾。 海岸共同體提供中立觀察員，核實城市已停止強制修正。 一棟擁擠宿舍的居民在了解醫療選項後，申請受監督的中繼停機。 報紙社論稱讚試驗通過，也提醒讀者所有權爭議仍未解決。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. Vale’s update disabled that clause but left a complete change log. Mira felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。維爾的更新關掉條款，卻留下完整修改紀錄。米拉一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The policy prohibited exclusion from repairs based on body size. The government's founding fable was losing power as residents read authenticated records. A fragrant bowl of soup reminded one survivor of home without requiring a forced memory session. A homosexual resident told the forum that personal identity and private memory both deserved protection. The original council had tried to industrialize the southern region by removing communities without consent. Mira asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 政策禁止因身形而排除維修服務。 居民閱讀認證紀錄後，政府的建城寓言逐漸失去力量。 一碗香噴噴的湯讓一名倖存者想起家，不必接受強制恢復。 一名同性戀居民向論壇表示，個人身分與私人記憶都值得保護。 原議會曾試圖在未經同意下搬遷社區，以將南部地區工業化。 米拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Mira had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Theo proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 米拉原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。西奧提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Ivy had grown over the sealed railway bridge, but its stone supports remained intact. A sparrow chose to perch on the window frame during the second alarm test. An old petroleum pipeline beneath the relay station required inspection before electrical isolation began. Vale's talk of mental purity had disguised a policy of fear, surveillance, and compulsory editing. A minor quake damaged two supply cables, delaying an isolation that might otherwise endanger hospital patients. Theo marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 常春藤覆蓋了封閉鐵路橋，石製橋墩卻仍完好。 第二次警報測試時，一隻麻雀停棲窗框。 中繼站下的一條舊石油管線，必須在電力隔離前接受檢查。 維爾口中的心智純淨，掩蓋了恐懼、監視與強制編輯政策。 一次小地震損壞兩條供電電纜，延後隔離程序，避免危及醫院病患。 西奧標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The public revolt took the form of petitions, legal challenges, and refusal to accept compulsory memory broadcasts. Rugged mechanical switches offered a safer emergency option than remotely controlled settings in damaged districts. Mira asked reporters not to skim the court ruling and turn its limited protection into an absolute claim. Children playing outdoors gave the lower neighborhood a vitality missing from official depictions of abandonment. Ivo continued to yearn for a reunion with his mother while honoring her request for time. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 這場公眾反抗以請願、法律挑戰與拒絕強制記憶廣播的形式進行。 在設備受損地區，堅固的機械開關比遠端控制設定提供更安全的緊急選項。 米拉請記者別草草瀏覽法院裁定，就把有限保障說成絕對結論。 在戶外玩耍的孩子，讓下層社區充滿活力，與官方描繪的荒廢景象不同。 伊沃仍渴望與母親重逢，同時尊重她需要時間的要求。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Mira returned to a library card with no network ID. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Theo remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the offline city of Haven waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，米拉再次看向一張沒有網路識別碼的借書證。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。西奧守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的離線城市港灣等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

## CHAPTER 21｜The Hearing Beyond the Cloud｜雲端之外的聽證

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：869**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. A joint tribunal accepts witnesses who appear in person, by audio, or through cloud projection. Mira checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。聯合法庭接受現場、音訊與雲端投影三種作證方式。米拉核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** A witness asked whether the inquiry would make tomorrow safer. Mira could not promise that. The truth could establish responsibility, but it would not repair a broken machine, feed a hungry child, or return lost time on its own. Theo suggested a temporary protection that could be checked after sunrise. They wrote down its limits in plain language and invited the people affected to challenge them.

**繁中** 一位證人問調查是否能讓明天更安全，米拉無法保證。真相能確立責任，卻不會自行修好機器、餵飽孩子或歸還失去的時間。西奧提出一項日出後可以檢驗的暫時保護措施。他們用白話寫清限制，邀請受影響的人提出異議。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The new clinic made every operator accountable to patients and an independent medical board. Mira used an analogy of a damaged library book to explain why restoration could not guarantee a perfect memory. The correction was announced in the school auditorium with public records available nearby. A cardinal rule of the audit was to preserve every original record before asking witnesses to interpret it. The nurse remained communicative throughout the procedure so the volunteer could understand each step. Mira asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 新診所讓每位操作人員都必須對病患與獨立醫療委員會負責。 米拉以受損圖書作比喻，說明恢復不保證得到完美記憶。 更正結果在學校禮堂宣布，附近可查閱公開紀錄。 稽核最重要的一項原則，是先保存所有原始紀錄，再請證人解釋。 護理師在整個療程中持續清楚溝通，讓志願者理解每個步驟。 米拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** For once the numbers did not merely decorate a report; they contradicted the order that had been issued in their name. It orders access restored while investigating whether Vale knowingly deprived residents of essential care. Theo asked whether a mistake could explain the result. Mira agreed to test that possibility too. They checked an independent source, marked every assumption, and made copies before the originals could be transferred to an office beyond public reach.

**繁中** 這一次數字不只是報告裝飾，而是直接反駁以數字名義發布的命令。法庭下令恢復服務，同時調查維爾是否明知故犯剝奪居民基本照護。西奧詢問是否可能只是出錯，米拉也同意檢查這種可能。他們核對獨立來源、標記每項假設，在原件被移往公眾無法接觸的辦公室前留下副本。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Nari called the elderly teacher a comrade in reform without expecting her to reveal her private memories. A volunteer reported a hand cramp during testing, and the clinician immediately paused the session. A past accident had forced the crew to hospitalize a worker, making accurate maintenance records more than an administrative concern. A monitor strap caused an itch on one patient's wrist, which the nurse recorded as a separate physical complaint. Lan said longevity mattered less to her than having a meaningful daily routine. Theo marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 娜莉稱年老教師是改革夥伴，卻不要求她揭露私人記憶。 一名志願者在測試時手部抽筋，醫師立刻暫停療程。 過去一次事故迫使工作隊將工人送醫住院，使正確保養紀錄不只是行政問題。 一名病患手腕因監測環帶而發癢，護理師把它另列為身體不適。 婉如說，對她而言，長壽不如每天過得有意義。 西奧標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Director Vale and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Mira rejected both. The issue was whether people have the right to remain real without joining a cloud, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住維爾總監的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。米拉兩者都拒絕。核心是人們能否不加入雲端也保有真實身分，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The teacher's decision to stop helped propel the clinic toward a stronger culture of consent. A student wrote a piece of prose about learning to enter the water. The clinician rejected the claim that patients had gained psychic powers merely because they described similar dreams. An independent referee reviewed disputes between archive custodians and residents requesting access. The reviewer was relentless about recording errors on both sides. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 教師決定停止療程，推動診所建立更強的同意文化。 一名學生寫了篇散文，描述學會入水的經驗。 臨床醫師拒絕說病患因描述相似夢境就獲得通靈能力。 一名獨立仲裁員審查檔案保管機構與申請查閱居民之間的爭議。 審查員堅持不懈地記錄雙方的錯誤。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A paper train ticket became a cherished relic, not proof that every passenger on its route had survived. Workers had to scrape loose paint away before applying the new protective coating. Visitors could scroll through public summaries without gaining access to anyone's sealed medical file. A child's red sneaker remained in a sealed evidence box because its owner had not consented to display. Officials had continued to underestimate residents' capacity to make informed decisions about their own memories. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 一張紙本車票成為珍貴遺物，卻不能證明路線上每名乘客都活著。 工人塗上新保護層前，必須刮除鬆動油漆。 訪客可以捲動閱讀公開摘要，卻不能看到任何人的密封病歷。 一隻兒童紅球鞋仍封存在證物箱裡，因為物主尚未同意展示。 官員一直低估居民對自身記憶做出知情選擇的能力。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Mira had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Theo wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Director Vale. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。米拉知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。西奧把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起維爾總監注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

   - C. It orders access restored while investigating whether Vale knowingly deprived residents of essential care.
