## CHAPTER 22｜The Roads Return｜道路重新出現

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：869**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. The city reappears on public maps after the exclusionary rule is suspended. Mira knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。排除規則暫停後，城市重新出現在公共地圖上。米拉知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Mira asked Theo not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。米拉請西奧不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The archive contained an abundance of handwritten logs, though only a fraction had yet been carefully cataloged. The guardian asked the team to avoid causing unnecessary distress to survivors named in the recovered files. A resident's fury about hidden repair costs was understandable, but the meeting required precise questions rather than threats. A children's costume designer packed a playful gorilla mask for the trip. Poor access to the original logs would hamper future research, so the trustees funded a searchable public index. Theo marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 檔案室有大量手寫日誌，但目前只有一小部分完成仔細編目。 監護人要求小組避免讓檔案上出現的倖存者承受不必要痛苦。 居民對隱藏修繕費用的憤怒可以理解，會議仍要求精確提問而非威脅。 童裝工藝師替旅行收進一張俏皮大猩猩面具。 無法取得原始日誌會妨礙未來研究，因此受託人資助可搜尋的公開索引。 西奧標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. Mira insists that appearing on a map must not require surrendering the contents of one’s mind. Mira watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Theo recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。米拉堅持出現在地圖上，不該以交出心智內容為代價。米拉看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。西奧公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A rusted harmonica found beside the shipping letters was listed as property, not used to infer a life story. The archive's privacy policy protected heterosexual and LGBTQ families equally when recording relationships. The slow audit could irritate impatient activists, but it reduced the risk of accusing innocent workers. Some witnesses chose to remain mute in public and submit private written testimony instead. The forged epidemic order appeared to originate from a council office that had controlled the first relocations. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 運輸書信旁找到一把生鏽口琴，被列為物品，而沒有用來猜測一個人的人生。 檔案隱私政策在記錄親密關係時，同等保障異性戀與 LGBTQ 家庭。 緩慢稽核可能使焦急倡議者不耐煩，卻降低誤控無辜工人的風險。 部分證人選擇在公開場合保持沉默，改交私人書面證詞。 偽造的疫情命令似乎源自控制第一批搬遷的議會辦公室。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Mira insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Theo drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。米拉堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。西奧畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A pony in the storybook carried a child's letter to the next village. The predecessor committee had failed to document how free hours were assigned. Residents could preview redacted archive summaries before deciding whether to request their own files. The original departure ledger was priceless to families seeking an accurate account of their relatives. Archivists worked to salvage water-damaged letters without replacing missing words with guesses. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 故事書裡的小馬把孩子的信送到鄰村。 前任委員會未記錄免費時段如何分配。 居民可先預覽刪去敏感資訊的檔案摘要，再決定是否申請自己的紀錄。 對尋求親人真實去向的家庭而言，原始出發名冊無價。 檔案員努力搶救受潮書信，沒有用猜測補上缺失文字。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A sneaky change to the archive index had concealed the vault's location for decades. The archive allowed donors to choose whether their surname appeared beside a public description of family photographs. A sudden torrent of rain flooded the service stair, forcing the team to suspend the archive transfer. A retired veterinarian identified animal transport stamps on a set of railway cargo records. A young violinist performed at the meeting's close, after trustees had recorded the difficult budget decisions in full. Mira asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 一項偷偷修改檔案索引的行為，使地庫位置被藏了幾十年。 檔案室讓捐贈者選擇家族照片公開說明旁是否顯示姓氏。 一陣暴雨急流淹過維修樓梯，迫使小組暫停檔案移交。 退休獸醫認出一批鐵路貨運紀錄上的動物運輸印章。 受託人完整記錄困難預算決議後，一名年輕小提琴家在會末演奏。 米拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Mira passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Theo paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，米拉經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。西奧停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

   - C. The city reappears on public maps after the exclusionary rule is suspended.

## CHAPTER 23｜Two Kinds of Home｜兩種家

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：841**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** By noon, rumors had outrun the facts. Some people wanted a villain; others wanted a hero who could end the argument without asking another question. Theo visits Haven in a physical service body and shares tea with Mira beside the old library steps. Mira had worked long enough to distrust both shortcuts. In the offline city of Haven, the first task was to protect the evidence before anyone could turn it into a weapon.

**繁中** 到了中午，謠言早已跑得比事實快。有人想找反派，也有人想找英雄，好在不再追問的情況下結束爭論。西奧透過實體服務載具到訪港灣，在舊圖書館階梯旁與米拉喝茶。米拉工作多年，已經不相信這兩種捷徑。在離線城市港灣，當務之急是先保護證據，免得任何人將它變成武器。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Mira listened longer than the schedule allowed. Theo reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。米拉聽得比議程允許的更久。西奧提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The court separated the railway collision reports from later claims that every missing person had died. The school planetarium announced a comet display on the same weekend as the meeting. The published inspection thresholds were meant to deter unsafe shortcuts, not punish a company for reporting an honest failure. The eccentric donor requested a seat on the board in return for cash. A trained escort accompanied elderly residents to the accessible entrance. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 法院把鐵路碰撞事故報告，與後來宣稱所有失蹤者都死亡的說法分開。 學校星象館宣布在會議同週末舉辦彗星展示。 公開檢查門檻旨在遏止不安全捷徑，而非懲罰誠實通報故障的公司。 古怪捐助者要求以金錢換取董事席位。 受訓陪同員帶年長居民前往無障礙入口。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. They disagree about uploading, but no longer let an index decide whether they are family. Mira read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Theo did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。他們對上傳仍有歧見，卻不再讓索引決定彼此是否為家人。米拉把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。西奧沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: An engineering examinee used the case to explain why safety reviews must include deployment conditions. A child's garment retained the stitched name of a school removed from official maps. The board separated navigation performance from ownership questions, although both would affect the tower's future. Ning's outlook changed from heroic rescue to the slower work of institutions that could be challenged. A surviving paperback novel contained a handwritten address from the vanished orchard district. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 一名工程考生以本案說明，安全審查為何須包含實際部署條件。 一件童衣仍縫著已從官方地圖移除的學校名稱。 委員會把導航效能與所有權問題分開，儘管兩者都會影響燈塔未來。 寧澄的觀點從英雄拯救，轉向可接受質疑的慢速制度工作。 一本倖存的平裝小說裡，寫著消失果園行政區的手寫地址。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Director Vale demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Mira replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當維爾總監要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。米拉回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The reporter's question could paralyze discussion if everyone feared private exposure. The judge said a patriot was not someone who protected a government from evidence of wrongdoing. A passerby tried to peek at the confidential aid applications; staff moved them away. A sorrowful account of a past shipwreck reminded the board why disputed safety records deserved serious attention. The rumor spread in a spiral of repeated comments before the correction appeared. Mira asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 若人人害怕私事曝光，記者的問題可能讓討論停擺。 法官說，愛國者不是替政府擋住不法證據的人。 一名路人試圖偷看保密補助申請，員工隨即把文件移開。 一段過去船難的悲傷敘述，提醒委員會為何必須認真面對有爭議的安全紀錄。 更正出現前，謠言在重複留言中螺旋擴散。 米拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: No subordinate should be ordered to falsify a medical record, regardless of who holds authority. The new regulations forbade operators to transmit memory-altering pulses without individualized medical consent. The artificial stars continued to twinkle above the courthouse after the ruling was announced. The old velvet curtain was removed from the exit because it blocked the signs. A nearby machine continued to vibrate until its operator switched it off. Theo marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 不論上級權勢多大，都不能命令下屬偽造病歷。 新規禁止未取得個別醫療同意就傳送改變記憶的脈衝。 判決宣布後，人造星星仍在法院上方閃爍。 舊天鵝絨簾子因遮住出口標誌而被移除。 附近一台機器持續震動，直到操作員將它關閉。 西奧標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Mira resisted the urge to call it proof. Theo opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。米拉克制住把它當成證明的衝動。西奧翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Theo visits Haven in a physical service body and shares tea with Mira beside the old library steps.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 西奧透過實體服務載具到訪港灣，在舊圖書館階梯旁與米拉喝茶。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. They disagree about uploading, but no longer let an index decide whether they are family.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 他們對上傳仍有歧見，卻不再讓索引決定彼此是否為家人。


## CHAPTER 24｜The Open Directory｜開放名錄

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：877**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The room smelled of dust, hot metal, and rain carried in on people's coats. Someone had left an ordinary object on the central table, yet no one wanted to touch it. Residents design a public directory that lists locations and services while keeping personal histories private. Mira saw that the object mattered less for what it was than for the decision it proved had been made. There would be no honest way back to ignorance.

**繁中** 室內有灰塵、熱金屬與人們外套帶進的雨味。有人把平凡物件放在中央桌上，卻沒有人願意碰它。居民設計公共名錄，列出地點和服務，同時保護個人歷史隱私。米拉明白重要的不是物件本身，而是它證明了何種決定已經發生。如今已不可能誠實地假裝無知。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** Theo arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Mira could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 西奧帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。米拉感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The real coastline extended beyond the walls farther than any public school map had shown. The new director would compile public reports from several village committees. The signed sketchbook would complement the verified delivery records. Ning took time to console a child disappointed by another temporary lane restriction. The new travel charter barred officials from using forgotten family records to detain innocent passengers. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 真正的海岸線延伸到城牆外，比任何公立學校地圖畫得更遠。 新主任將彙整數個村委會的公開報告。 有簽名的草圖簿可補足經查證的交貨紀錄。 宋穎花時間安慰因再次限制水道而失望的孩子。 新旅行章程禁止官員利用被遺忘的家屬紀錄拘留無辜乘客。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. The project is maintained by both uploaded and offline volunteers with transparent oversight. For a long moment no one spoke. Mira understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Theo broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。計畫由上傳者與離線志工共同維護，接受透明監督。一時之間無人開口。米拉明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。西奧打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Diplomacy between Everday and the local station made the first supervised passenger service possible. The audit traced a downward adjustment in the reported repair costs. the office council adopted drastic changes only after independent review. The authorities avoided a drastic reopening before the damaged bridges passed independent tests. The coastal museum displayed a glacier survey that proved the outside world still had active scientific institutions. Mira refused to treat the new coastal reports as gospel; they too required verification and public challenge. Mira asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 歷史修正局與九號港之間的外交協商，使首班受監督客運成為可能。 辦公場所議會經獨立審查後才採取劇烈改革。 當局避免在受損橋梁通過獨立檢查前，進行激烈全面開放。 海岸博物館展示一份冰河測量，證明外部世界仍有運作中的科學機構。 米拉拒絕把新的海岸報告當成絕對真理，它們也需要驗證與公開質疑。 米拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Mira had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Theo proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 米拉原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。西奧提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Security staff had procedures to prevent anyone from attempting to hijack the first passenger train. Ning admitted she would be a hypocrite if she demanded transparent records while hiding her own testing mistakes. A familiar station jingle played as the repaired train approached Harbor Nine. Ivo admitted he had felt lonesome for years, though he did not expect Mira to repair that loss for him. The new map printed accurate longitude markings for towns once erased from public navigation systems. Theo marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 保全人員有程序，防止有人企圖劫持首班客車。 寧澄承認，若要求別人透明、自己卻藏起測試錯誤，就是偽善者。 修復列車接近九號港時，響起熟悉的車站短音樂。 伊沃承認多年來感到孤單，卻不期待米拉替他修復所有失落。 新地圖為昔日從公共導航系統抹除的城鎮印上準確經度標記。 西奧標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** Mira unlocked a cabinet with a metal key and checked the handwritten list of borrowers. The paper did not know whether the people named on it were fashionable, connected, or profitable. Theo could still recall the smell of the old library, though the cloud index insisted that the building had never stood there.

**繁中** 米拉用金屬鑰匙打開櫃子，核對手寫借書名單。紙張不在乎名單上的人是否時髦、連線或有利可圖。西奧仍記得舊圖書館的氣味，雖然雲端索引堅稱那棟樓從未存在。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The storm had once produced monstrous waves here, but the new chart described the hazards in measured terms. The historical archive documented oppression of junior factory workers. The newly surveyed reef remained marked on the chart even after the navigation beacon passed its tests. The two cities promised solidarity through practical rights, not a forced declaration of shared identity. The new commission could not become another vice gripping citizens' private memories in the name of safety. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 這裡曾出現可怕巨浪，新海圖卻以可衡量方式描述危險。 歷史檔案記錄年輕辦公場所工坊員工受到的壓迫。 即使導航燈通過測試，新測礁石仍保留標示在海圖上。 兩座城市承諾以實際權利展現團結，而非強迫宣告共同身分。 新委員會不能再以安全之名，成為箝制居民私人記憶的惡習與束縛。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Mira returned to a library card with no network ID. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Theo remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the offline city of Haven waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，米拉再次看向一張沒有網路識別碼的借書證。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。西奧守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的離線城市港灣等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. Residents design a public directory that lists locations and services while keeping personal histories private.
   - C. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 居民設計公共名錄，列出地點和服務，同時保護個人歷史隱私。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The project is maintained by both uploaded and offline volunteers with transparent oversight.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 計畫由上傳者與離線志工共同維護，接受透明監督。


## CHAPTER 25｜The City That Could Be Remembered｜能夠被記住的城市

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：873**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Nobody had called it a crisis at first. A clerk had used a harmless word, a guard had followed an ordinary instruction, and by evening somebody else was carrying the cost. Mira refused to begin with the official conclusion. Mira renews a child's library card without asking for a cloud identity number. In the offline city of Haven, even an accurate number could become a lie when it left out the person whose life it counted.

**繁中** 起初沒有人稱這是危機。書記使用看似無害的詞，守衛照普通命令行事，到了晚上代價卻落在別人身上。米拉拒絕從官方結論開始。米拉替孩子續借書證，沒有索取雲端身分號碼。在離線城市港灣，精確的數字若遺漏真正承受後果的人，也可能成為謊言。

**EN** The argument was not merely about who held authority. It was about what happened when an official decision met a household, a workplace, or a body that could not obey it safely. Mira asked Theo to describe the smallest practical consequence. Only then did they realize that the proposed remedy would reproduce the very harm it claimed to prevent. They changed the plan before presenting it in public.

**繁中** 爭論不只是誰有權威，而是官方決定落到家庭、職場或無法安全服從的身體上時會發生什麼。米拉請西奧說明最小、最實際的後果。他們這才發現提議中的補救會重複它聲稱要防止的傷害，因此在公開發表前修改了計畫。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: A swimmer with asthma asked for a lesson plan compatible with her clinician's advice. A bilateral agreement between Everday and the local station protected voluntary family visits and data privacy. The new director met her counterpart from another valley to coordinate emergency support. The city kept its diesel backup generator under a documented inspection schedule. The clinic stopped using disposable sensor bands when reusable alternatives passed medical safety tests. Mira asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 一名氣喘泳者要求課程能配合臨床專業人員的建議。 歷史修正局與九號港的雙邊協議，保障自願探親與資料隱私。 新站長與另一座山谷的對口主管協調緊急支援。 市府依書面檢查時程保養柴油備援發電機。 可重複使用的感測環通過醫療安全測試後，診所不再使用拋棄式環帶。 米拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. Outside, an ambulance stops at the correct address, and the city remains free to remember itself in more than one way. Mira felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。窗外救護車停在正確地址，城市終於能自由選擇不只一種記住自己的方式。米拉一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Officials could no longer divert requests for missing-person records into a general category of sleep complaints. Mira could doze peacefully on the afternoon train without expecting an official alarm to punish her dreams. A friend joked that Ivo was Mira's fiancé, but Mira replied that they had made no such announcement. The archive trust depended on enforceable rules as well as goodwill among former opponents. The new archive used a hybrid system of physical originals and verified digital copies. Theo marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 官員不能再把尋找失蹤者紀錄的申請，轉送到一般睡眠申訴類別。 米拉能在午後列車上安心打盹，不再預期官方警報會懲罰她的夢。 朋友開玩笑稱伊沃是米拉未婚夫，米拉回答兩人從未宣布訂婚。 檔案信託除了仰賴昔日對手的善意，也需要可執行的規則。 新檔案館採用紙本正本與可驗證數位副本的混合制度。 西奧標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Director Vale and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Mira rejected both. The issue was whether people have the right to remain real without joining a cloud, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住維爾總監的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。米拉兩者都拒絕。核心是人們能否不加入雲端也保有真實身分，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: An old lad from Orchard East returned as an elderly man to see the street restored to the map. The repaired first-aid case had a fresh lining beneath its worn outer cover. A handwritten manuscript of the neighborhood's history was added to the public library with its author's consent. Public outrage helped start the inquiry, but patient verification made the reforms durable. Mira saw the paradox of her profession: a technology built to relieve pain had been used to deny reality. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 一名東果園昔日的少年如今已是老人，回來看街道重新出現在地圖上。 修好的急救箱在磨損外殼下換上新內襯。 一份手寫社區歷史稿在作者同意下納入公共圖書館。 公眾憤怒促成調查，耐心驗證才讓改革得以長久。 米拉看見自己專業的悖論：為減輕痛苦而建的技術，竟被用來否認現實。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The town helped rehabilitate the old weather classroom so future keepers could train without closing the public path. A renowned historian helped verify the original railway dates but did not control the public archive. Residents chose to revive the orchard festival with new traditions rather than recreate an imagined perfect past. Underneath the new public map lay layers of streets whose histories the city finally allowed people to study. The agreement rejected forced unification of the cities and protected each community's self-government. A coastal villa once used by senior officials was converted into a public center for reunion counseling. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 小鎮協助修復舊氣象教室，讓未來守護員不必封閉公共步道也能受訓。 一位著名歷史學家協助核實原始鐵路日期，卻不掌控公共檔案館。 居民選擇以新傳統恢復果園節，而不是重建想像中完美的過去。 新公共地圖底下有一層又一層街道歷史，如今城市終於允許人們研究。 協議拒絕強制統一兩座城市，保障各社區自治。 一棟曾供高階官員使用的海岸別墅，改成公共家屬重逢諮詢中心。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Mira had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Theo wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Director Vale. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。米拉知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。西奧把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起維爾總監注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

**EN** Mira did not call the day a perfect victory. Outside, an ambulance stops at the correct address, and the city remains free to remember itself in more than one way. What endured was a decision that the people of the offline city of Haven could challenge, revise, and make their own. Theo placed a library card with no network ID beside the public record. They left the last page open for names not yet written.

**繁中** 米拉不把這一天稱為完美勝利。窗外救護車停在正確地址，城市終於能自由選擇不只一種記住自己的方式。真正留下的是離線城市港灣人民有權質疑、修改並共同擁有的決定。西奧把一張沒有網路識別碼的借書證放在公共紀錄旁。他們把最後一頁留白，等待未來尚未寫上的姓名。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. Mira renews a child's library card without asking for a cloud identity number.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 米拉替孩子續借書證，沒有索取雲端身分號碼。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. Outside, an ambulance stops at the correct address, and the city remains free to remember itself in more than one way.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 窗外救護車停在正確地址，城市終於能自由選擇不只一種記住自己的方式。
